We propose a diffusion-based frame work that improves multi-agent motion prediction by lever aging rich contextual information from historical trajecto ries. This information is incorporated through a guidance mechanism to enhance the diversity and expressiveness of predicted motions. To further enforce interaction consis tency, we introduce an energy-based formulation that re fines the joint trajectory distribution while preserving the plausibility of individual trajectories. Extensive experi ments on four benchmark datasets demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms existing methods. No tably, our approach substantially improves both marginal (ADE/FDE) and joint (JADE/JFDE) metrics on ETH/UCY over strong marginal baselines. Compared with prior joint prediction methods, it delivers significant gains in marginal metrics while maintaining competitive joint performance.

中文摘要翻译: 深度生成模型因其捕捉多模态分布和表示多样化人类行为的能力，已成为人类运动预测领域的一种有前景的方法。然而，如何在交互智能体之间生成既多样又联合一致的预测仍然具有挑战性。此外，现有大多数方法主要使用单智能体（边际）指标进行评估，这未能充分反映多智能体交互的联合动态特性。我们提出了一种基于扩散的框架，通过利用历史轨迹中的丰富上下文信息来改进多智能体运动预测。该信息通过一种引导机制被引入，以增强预测运动的多样性和表现力。为了进一步强化交互一致性，我们引入了一种基于能量的公式，该公式在保持个体轨迹合理性的同时，对联合轨迹分布进行精炼。在四个基准数据集上进行的大量实验表明，我们的方法始终优于现有方法。值得注意的是，在ETH/UCY数据集上，我们的方法在边际指标（ADE/FDE）和联合指标（JADE/JFDE）上相较于强边际基线均有显著提升。与现有的联合预测方法相比，它在保持有竞争力的联合性能的同时，在边际指标上取得了显著提升。

</details>


### [30] [FRED: A Multi-Modal Autonomous Driving Dataset for Flooded Road Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22018)
*Connor Malone,Sebastien Demmel,Sebastien Glaser*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FRED是首个面向道路洪水场景的多模态自动驾驶数据集，包含图像、点云、IMU/GNSS数据及语义标签，支持水险检测、定位与SLAM等任务。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自动驾驶数据集缺乏针对道路积水/洪水场景的专门数据，阻碍了水险检测算法的研发与评估。

Method: 使用FLIR相机、Ouster LiDAR和iXblue IMU/GNSS在5个地点采集洪水期间及之后的道路数据，提供KITTI和RTMaps两种格式，并标注语义标签。

Result: 发布了首个多模态道路洪水数据集，包含图像、64线点云、位姿速度信息及干地对比数据，支持单传感器与融合方法的水险检测评估。

Conclusion: FRED数据集填补了自动驾驶领域道路水险场景数据的空白，为水险检测、定位与SLAM等研究提供了标准化基准。

Original Abstract: The Flooded Road Environments Dataset (FRED) is, to our knowledge, the first multi-modal autonomous driving dataset specifically targeting the collection of data from scenarios involving water hazards on the road. The dataset contains images from a 2.3 MP FLIR Blackfly USB3 camera, 64-beam 360$^\circ$ point clouds from an Ouster OS1-64 LiDAR, and data from an iXblue ATLANS-C IMU corrected by a Geoflex RTK GNSS, from five separate locations captured both during and after flooding events. The data has been released in two formats: a KITTI-style format for easy integration with existing data tools, and the RTMaps format for direct replay of the vehicle's data capture. We provide semantic labels to enable the training and evaluation of both single-sensor and sensor-fusion methods for water hazard detection. Position and velocity, as well as data captured under dry conditions, are provided to enable the development of location-based detection methods that may incorporate maps, and to evaluate other tasks such as localisation and SLAM.

中文摘要翻译: 据我们所知，洪水道路环境数据集（FRED）是首个专门针对道路涉水险情场景数据采集的多模态自动驾驶数据集。该数据集包含来自2.3 MP FLIR Blackfly USB3相机的图像、Ouster OS1-64激光雷达的64线360°点云，以及经Geoflex RTK GNSS校正的iXblue ATLANS-C IMU数据，采集自洪水事件期间及之后的五个不同地点。数据以两种格式发布：KITTI风格格式以方便与现有数据工具集成，以及RTMaps格式用于直接回放车辆数据采集过程。我们提供语义标签以支持水险检测的单传感器和传感器融合方法的训练与评估。同时提供位置与速度信息以及干燥条件下采集的数据，以支持可能结合地图的基于位置的检测方法开发，并评估其他任务如定位与SLAM。

</details>


### [31] [SO-Mamba: State-Ownership Mamba for Unrolled MRI Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22031)
*Pengcheng Fang,Hongli Chen,Fangfang Tang,Feng Liu,Xiaohao Cai,Shanshan Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SO-Mamba，一种状态归属Mamba正则化器，通过状态归属路由器（SOR）将重建证据分为循环驻留、状态接口访问和非状态输出校正三类，解决了展开式MRI重建中不同阶段证据混淆的问题，在五个公开基准上优于CNN、Transformer和Mamba基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在数据一致性耦合的展开式求解器中，不同阶段处理不同的重建迭代结果：驻留载体（resident carrier）需要在各阶段间保持连贯的重建内容，而非驻留证据（non-resident evidence）仅与当前更新相关。现有方法将这两类证据统一处理，导致持久性驻留载体证据和更新依赖的非驻留证据被混淆在同一条循环内容路径中，影响重建质量。

Method: 提出状态归属Mamba正则化器（SO-Mamba），核心是状态归属路由器（SOR）：(1) 将重建证据分为循环驻留（resident carrier）、状态接口访问（state-interface access）和非状态输出校正（non-state output correction）三类；(2) 驻留载体为循环内容提供路径，非驻留证据通过仿射调制影响B/C状态接口，并通过输出校正出口贡献，但不进入循环内容路径。此外引入两级外带泄漏诊断（two-level outer-band leakage diagnostic），通过测量选择扫描状态轨迹和后扫描Mamba读出的外带能量来分离隐藏状态存储与读出表达。

Result: 在五个涵盖不同解剖结构、采样模式和线圈配置的公开MRI重建基准上，SO-Mamba一致优于基于CNN、Transformer和Mamba的基线方法，且计算效率具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过状态归属机制明确区分驻留与非驻留证据的传播路径，能够更有效地利用Mamba的长程建模能力提升加速MRI重建质量。

Original Abstract: Accelerated MRI reconstruction requires recovering missing details while preserving anatomically coherent structures across large spatial regions. State-space models such as Mamba provide efficient long-range modeling, making them attractive learned regularizers for unrolled reconstruction. However, in a data-consistency-coupled unrolled solver, different stages operate on different reconstruction iterates, where the resident carrier should preserve coherent reconstruction content across stages while stage-dependent non-resident evidence is tied to the current update. Treating these roles uniformly can place persistent resident-carrier evidence and update-dependent non-resident evidence into the same recurrent content route. We therefore propose SO-Mamba, a state-ownership Mamba regularizer that assigns reconstruction evidence within each Mamba stage to recurrent residency, state-interface access, and non-state output correction. SO-Mamba implements this ownership rule with a State-Ownership Router (SOR), which constructs a resident carrier for recurrent content and routes non-resident evidence to affine modulation of the B/C state interfaces and an output correction outlet. The resident carrier supplies the Mamba content route, while the non-resident evidence stream adapts the state interfaces and contributes through the output outlet without entering the recurrent content route. We further introduce a two-level outer-band leakage diagnostic that separates hidden-state storage from readout expression by measuring outer-band energy in the selective-scan state trajectory and the post-scan Mamba readout. Experiments on five public MRI reconstruction benchmarks spanning diverse anatomies, sampling patterns, and coil configurations show that SO-Mamba consistently improves over CNN-, Transformer-, and Mamba-based baselines with competitive computational efficiency.

中文摘要翻译: 加速MRI重建需要在恢复缺失细节的同时，跨大空间区域保持解剖结构的一致性。状态空间模型（如Mamba）因其高效的长程建模能力，成为展开式重建中具有吸引力的可学习正则化器。然而，在数据一致性耦合的展开式求解器中，不同阶段处理不同的重建迭代结果：驻留载体应在各阶段间保持连贯的重建内容，而依赖于更新阶段的非驻留证据则与当前更新绑定。将这些角色统一对待会将持久性驻留载体证据和更新依赖的非驻留证据放入同一条循环内容路径中。为此，我们提出SO-Mamba，一种状态归属Mamba正则化器，将每个Mamba阶段内的重建证据划分为循环驻留、状态接口访问和非状态输出校正三类。SO-Mamba通过状态归属路由器（SOR）实现这一归属规则：构建用于循环内容的驻留载体，并将非驻留证据路由至B/C状态接口的仿射调制以及输出校正出口。驻留载体为Mamba内容路径提供信息，而非驻留证据流则适配状态接口并通过输出出口贡献，不进入循环内容路径。我们进一步引入两级外带泄漏诊断，通过测量选择扫描状态轨迹和后扫描Mamba读出的外带能量，将隐藏状态存储与读出表达相分离。在五个涵盖不同解剖结构、采样模式和线圈配置的公开MRI重建基准上的实验表明，SO-Mamba一致优于基于CNN、Transformer和Mamba的基线方法，且计算效率具有竞争力。

</details>


### [32] [AgroVG: A Large-Scale Multi-Source Benchmark for Agricultural Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22034)
*Haocheng Li,Juepeng Zheng,Zenghao Yang,Kaiqi Du,Guilong Xiao,Gengmeng Pu,Haohuan Fu,Jianxi Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AgroVG，一个面向农业视觉定位的多源基准测试，将农业定位建模为广义集合预测任务，涵盖单目标、多目标和目标缺失三种场景，包含10,071个标注图像-查询对。对26种模型配置的零样本评估显示，最优多目标Set-F1仅0.35，最优正查询掩码成功率在IoU@0.75下不足0.17，揭示当前模型在农业场景下的显著性能差距。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 农业AI系统（如选择性除草、病害监测、靶向收割）需要视觉定位能力，但农业目标具有小、重复、遮挡、形状不规则等特点，且指代表达可能涉及一个、多个或零个目标。现有基准难以同时评估定位精度、目标集完整性和无目标时的拒识能力，因此需要构建一个全面评估农业视觉定位的标准化基准。

Method: 提出AgroVG基准，将农业视觉定位形式化为广义集合预测任务：给定图像和指代表达，模型需返回所有匹配的目标实例，若无目标则拒识。基准整合10个源数据集的10,071个图像-查询对，涵盖6类农业目标（作物/杂草、水果、麦穗、害虫、植物病害、树冠）。支持边界框定位（T1，全部6类）和实例掩码定位（T2，有可靠像素标注的源数据集），查询覆盖单目标、多目标和无目标三种情况。提供框集匹配和查询级掩码覆盖的任务评估协议。

Result: 对26种模型配置（涵盖闭源多模态大模型、开源视觉语言模型和专用定位系统）进行零样本评估，结果显示：最优多目标Set-F1仅0.35，最优正查询掩码成功率在IoU@0.75下低于0.17，表明现有模型在农业场景下的视觉定位能力存在显著不足。

Conclusion: 当前主流视觉定位模型在农业场景下表现不佳，尤其是多目标定位和精确掩码分割方面存在显著差距，AgroVG基准为未来农业视觉定位研究提供了标准化的评估平台。

Original Abstract: Visual grounding, the task of localizing objects described by natural-language expressions, is a foundational capability for agricultural AI systems, enabling applications such as selective weeding, disease monitoring, and targeted harvesting. Reliable evaluation of agricultural visual grounding remains challenging because agricultural targets are often small, repetitive, occluded, or irregularly shaped, and instructions may refer to one, many, or no objects in an image. Evaluating this capability therefore requires jointly testing localization accuracy, target-set completeness, and existence-aware abstention. To address these challenges, we introduce \textbf{AgroVG}, a multi-source benchmark that formulates agricultural grounding as generalized set prediction: given an image and a referring expression, a model must return all matching target instances or abstain when no target is present. AgroVG contains 10{,}071 annotation-grounded image-query pairs from ten source datasets across six target families: crop/weed, fruit, wheat head, pest, plant disease, and tree canopy. It supports bounding-box grounding (T1) across all six families and instance-mask grounding (T2) on sources with reliable instance-level pixel annotations, with queries covering single-target, multi-target, and target-absent regimes. AgroVG further provides task-specific protocols for box-set matching and query-level mask coverage. Zero-shot evaluation of 26 model configurations spanning closed-source MLLMs, open-source VLMs, and specialized grounding systems reveals persistent gaps: the best multi-target Set-$F_1$ reaches only 0.35, and the best positive-query mask success rate at IoU@0.75 remains below 0.17. Data and code are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AgroVG-5172/ .

中文摘要翻译: 视觉定位（Visual Grounding），即定位自然语言表达式所描述对象的任务，是农业AI系统的基础能力，可支持选择性除草、病害监测和靶向收割等应用。由于农业目标通常具有小尺寸、重复性、遮挡或不规则形状等特点，且指代指令可能涉及图像中一个、多个或零个对象，农业视觉定位的可靠评估仍然具有挑战性。因此，评估该能力需要同时检验定位精度、目标集完整性和存在感知拒识能力。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了\textbf{AgroVG}，一个多源基准测试，将农业定位形式化为广义集合预测问题：给定一张图像和一个指代表达式，模型必须返回所有匹配的目标实例，若无目标则拒识。AgroVG包含来自10个源数据集的10,071个标注图像-查询对，覆盖6类目标：作物/杂草、水果、麦穗、害虫、植物病害和树冠。该基准支持全部6类的边界框定位（T1），以及在具有可靠实例级像素标注的数据源上进行实例掩码定位（T2），查询涵盖单目标、多目标和无目标三种模式。AgroVG还提供了针对框集匹配和查询级掩码覆盖的任务特定评估协议。对涵盖闭源多模态大语言模型、开源视觉语言模型和专用定位系统在内的26种模型配置进行零样本评估，揭示了持续存在的性能差距：最优多目标Set-F1仅达到0.35，且在IoU@0.75下最优正查询掩码成功率仍低于0.17。数据和代码已开源：https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AgroVG-5172/。

</details>


### [33] [GA-VLN: Geometry-Aware BEV Representation for Efficient Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22036)
*Jiahao Yang,Zihan Wang,Xiangyang Li,Xing Zhu,Yujun Shen,Yinghao Xu,Shuqiang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出几何感知BEV（GA-BEV）特征表示，通过显式深度投影和隐式3D先验融合，减少RGB视频的冗余token，提升VLN任务的空间推理效率和性能，无需DAgger增强或混合VQA训练即达到SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VLN方法依赖密集RGB视频，产生过多patch token且缺乏显式空间结构，导致计算开销大、空间推理能力有限。

Method: 从RGB-D输入构建BEV空间地图：将视觉特征投影到3D空间并按智能体中心布局聚合，保持几何一致性的同时减少token冗余；进一步融入预训练3D基础模型的特征，注入大规模3D重建任务中学到的结构先验。

Result: 仅使用导航数据（无需DAgger增强或混合VQA训练）即达到SOTA结果，证明了框架的鲁棒性和数据效率。

Conclusion: 显式深度投影与隐式学习先验的互补融合能生成紧凑且空间表达力强的特征表示，显著提升导航效率与性能。

Original Abstract: Despite significant progress in Vision-Language Navigation (VLN), existing approaches still rely on dense RGB videos that produce excessive patch tokens and lack explicit spatial structure, resulting in substantial computational overhead and limited spatial reasoning. To address these issues, we introduce the Geometry-Aware BEV (GA-BEV) - a compact, 3D-grounded feature representation that integrates both explicit and implicit geometric cues into multimodal large language model (MLLM) - based navigation systems. We construct BEV spatial maps from RGB-D inputs by projecting visual features into 3D space and aggregating them into an agent-centric layout that preserves geometric consistency while reducing token redundancy. To further enrich geometric understanding, we incorporate features from a pretrained 3D foundation model into the BEV space, injecting structural priors learned from large-scale 3D reconstruction tasks. Together, these complementary cues - explicit depth-based projection and implicit learned priors - yield compact yet spatially expressive representations that substantially improve navigation efficiency and performance. Experiments show that our method achieves state-of-the-art results using only navigation data, without DAgger augmentation or mixed VQA training, demonstrating the robustness and data efficiency of the proposed GA-VLN framework.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管视觉-语言导航（VLN）取得了显著进展，现有方法仍依赖密集的RGB视频，产生大量patch token且缺乏显式空间结构，导致计算开销大、空间推理能力有限。为解决这些问题，我们引入了几何感知BEV（GA-BEV）——一种紧凑的、基于3D的特征表示，它将显式和隐式几何线索融入基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的导航系统中。我们通过将视觉特征投影到3D空间并聚合为智能体中心布局，从RGB-D输入构建BEV空间地图，在保持几何一致性的同时减少token冗余。为进一步增强几何理解，我们将预训练3D基础模型的特征融入BEV空间，注入从大规模3D重建任务中学到的结构先验。这些互补线索——显式的基于深度投影和隐式的学习先验——共同生成紧凑且空间表达力强的表示，显著提升了导航效率和性能。实验表明，我们的方法仅使用导航数据（无需DAgger增强或混合VQA训练）即达到最先进的结果，展示了所提出的GA-VLN框架的鲁棒性和数据效率。

</details>


### [34] [Broken Memories: Detecting and Mitigating Memorization in Diffusion Models with Degraded Generations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22050)
*Yuanmin Huang,Mi Zhang,Chen Chen,Feifei Li,Geng Hong,Xiaoyu You,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次发现扩散模型中的记忆化会导致内部数值不稳定（表现为“破碎”伪影），并基于数值稳定性分析引入经验稳定区域来量化生成过程中的稳定行为，从而提出了一种逐步骤检测和自适应缓解的在线框架，在不改变提示或引导条件的情况下有效抑制记忆化，并在Stable Diffusion 1.4上实现了AUC>0.999的检测性能和0.0%的记忆化率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 扩散模型在生成高质量图像的同时存在记忆训练数据的严重隐私和版权风险，现有方法缺乏对记忆化内在机制的深入理解，亟需一种既能检测又能缓解记忆化且不影响生成语义保真度和图像质量的原则性方法。

Method: 受数值方法中稳定性分析的启发，基于潜变量更新范数引入经验稳定区域来定量刻画生成过程中的稳定行为，并据此提出一个原则性的在线框架，实现逐步骤的检测和自适应缓解记忆化，无需修改提示词或引导条件。

Result: 在Stable Diffusion 1.4上的大量实验表明：检测性能AUC>0.999，缓解后记忆化率为0.0%，且额外开销极低（每张图像约0.01秒）。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于数值稳定性分析的在线检测与缓解框架能够高效、精确地消除扩散模型中的记忆化现象，同时保持生成图像的语义保真度和质量，为隐私保护和版权合规提供了实用解决方案。

Original Abstract: While diffusion models excel at generating high-quality images, their tendency to memorize training data poses significant privacy and copyright risks. In this work, we for the first time identify that memorization induces internal numerical instability, often manifesting as visually ``broken'' artifacts. Inspired by stability analysis in numerical methods, we introduce empirical stability regions based on latent update norms to quantitatively characterize stable behavior during generation. Leveraging this, we propose a principled, on-the-fly framework for step-wise detection and adaptive mitigation. Our approach suppresses memorization without altering prompts or guidance, thereby preserving semantic fidelity and image quality. Extensive experiments on Stable Diffusion 1.4 demonstrate that our method achieves an AUC $>0.999$ detection performance and a $0.0\%$ memorization rate after mitigation with negligible overhead ($\approx0.01$s per image).

中文摘要翻译: 虽然扩散模型在生成高质量图像方面表现出色，但其记忆训练数据的倾向带来了严重的隐私和版权风险。在这项工作中，我们首次发现记忆化会导致内部数值不稳定性，通常表现为视觉上的“破碎”伪影。受数值方法中稳定性分析的启发，我们基于潜变量更新范数引入了经验稳定区域，以定量刻画生成过程中的稳定行为。利用这一点，我们提出了一个原则性的在线框架，用于逐步骤检测和自适应缓解。我们的方法在不改变提示词或引导条件的情况下抑制记忆化，从而保持了语义保真度和图像质量。在Stable Diffusion 1.4上的大量实验表明，我们的方法实现了AUC>0.999的检测性能，缓解后记忆化率为0.0%，且额外开销可忽略不计（每张图像约0.01秒）。

</details>


### [35] [EasyVFX: Frequency-Driven Decoupling for Resource-Efficient VFX Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22051)
*Yue Ma,Xu Ye,Qinghe Wang,Yucheng Wang,Hongyu Liu,Yinhan Zhang,Xinyu Wang,Yuanpeng Che,Shanhui Mo,Paul Liang,Fangneng Zhan,Qifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EasyVFX提出了一种资源高效的视觉特效合成框架，通过频域分解将高维学习问题分解为可管理的子任务，并采用频率感知的混合专家架构（Freq-MoE）和测试时训练策略，在有限GPU资源下实现逼真的VFX生成。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 生成高质量视觉特效通常需要海量数据集和巨大的计算资源，因为空间纹理与时间动态之间存在复杂的耦合关系。现有方法资源消耗过高，限制了专业级VFX的普及。

Method: 提出两阶段训练范式：1）频域分解理念，将高频成分（精细空间外观）与低频成分（全局运动动态）解耦；2）频率感知混合专家架构（Freq-MoE），通过软路由机制为不同频谱带分配专门专家，学习外观与运动动态的鲁棒先验；3）测试时训练策略，结合新型频率约束损失，通过约100步局部优化使预训练模型快速适配未见特效。

Result: 实验结果表明，EasyVFX能够生成结构一致且视觉惊艳的特效，在极少的GPU资源下（单GPU、约100步优化）即可完成对新特效的快速适配，证明了频率感知学习是推动专业级VFX民主化的关键催化剂。

Conclusion: 频率感知的频域分解方法可以显著降低VFX合成的数据与计算依赖性，通过解耦空间外观与运动动态，将高维学习问题简化为子任务，实现了资源高效的专业级视觉特效合成。

Original Abstract: Generating high-fidelity visual effects (VFX) typically demands massive datasets and prohibitive computational power due to the intricate coupling of spatial textures and temporal dynamics. In this paper, we introduce EasyVFX, a resource-efficient framework that achieves realistic VFX synthesis under stringent constraints. Our core philosophy lies in frequency-domain decomposition: we observe that the complexity of VFX can be significantly mitigated by decoupling high-frequency components, which represent intricate spatial appearances, from low-frequency components that encapsulate global motion dynamics. This spectral disentanglement transforms a high-dimensional learning problem into manageable sub-tasks, thereby lowering the optimization barrier and reducing data dependency. Building upon this insight, we propose a two-stage training paradigm. First, we design a Frequency-aware Mixture-of-Experts (Freq-MoE) architecture. By utilizing a soft routing mechanism, our model assigns specialized experts to distinct spectral bands, enabling them to cultivate robust priors for appearance and motion dynamics. This specialization allows the model to acquire foundational VFX knowledge with fewer GPU resources. Second, we introduce a Test-Time Training strategy powered by a novel Frequency-constraint Loss. This allows the pre-trained model to swiftly adapt to specific, unseen effects through localized optimizations, requiring only about 100 steps on a single GPU. Experimental results demonstrate that EasyVFX produces structurally consistent and visually stunning effects, proving that frequency-aware learning is a key catalyst for democratizing professional-grade VFX.

中文摘要翻译: 生成高保真视觉特效通常需要海量数据集和巨大的计算资源，原因在于空间纹理与时间动态之间存在复杂的耦合关系。本文提出EasyVFX，一种资源高效的框架，能够在严格资源约束下实现逼真的视觉特效合成。我们的核心理念在于频域分解：我们观察到，通过将代表精细空间外观的高频成分与封装全局运动动态的低频成分解耦，可以显著降低VFX的复杂性。这种频谱解耦将高维学习问题转化为可管理的子任务，从而降低优化壁垒并减少数据依赖性。基于这一洞察，我们提出两阶段训练范式。首先，我们设计了频率感知混合专家架构。通过利用软路由机制，我们的模型为不同的频谱带分配专门的专家，使其能够培养外观与运动动态的鲁棒先验。这种专门化使得模型能够在更少的GPU资源下习得基础的VFX知识。其次，我们引入了一种测试时训练策略，由新颖的频率约束损失驱动。这使得预训练模型能够通过局部优化快速适配特定的、未见过的特效，仅需在单个GPU上执行约100步。实验结果表明，EasyVFX能够生成结构一致且视觉惊艳的特效，证明了频率感知学习是推动专业级VFX民主化的关键催化剂。

</details>


### [36] [Distributed Image Compression with Multimodal Side Information at Extremely Low Bitrates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22061)
*Guojun Xu,Mingyang Zhang,Jianwen Xiang,Cheng Tan,Yanchao Yang,Junwei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多模态分布式图像压缩框架（MDIC），首次以多模态方式利用侧信息，通过文本到图像的扩散解码器和特征掩码生成器，在极低比特率（<0.1 bpp）下实现高质量图像重建，在KITTI Stereo和Cityscapes数据集上达到最优感知质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有分布式图像压缩方法在极低比特率下难以有效利用侧信息中的全局上下文和物体级细节，导致重建图像出现局部模糊和细节丢失。

Method: 提出MDIC框架：(1) 基于文本到图像的扩散解码器，利用从相关图像中提取的文本侧信息捕获共享全局语义；(2) 特征掩码生成器，通过多模态细粒度对齐任务监督，增强视觉侧信息利用；生成的掩码一方面指导无损传输侧信息中的细节提取以保持语义一致性，另一方面调节从量化VQ-VAE嵌入中提取聚类特征表示，补偿极端压缩下丢失的类别信息。

Result: 在KITTI Stereo和Cityscapes数据集上的大量实验表明，MDIC在极低比特率下达到了最优的感知质量。

Conclusion: 多模态方式融合侧信息能有效提升分布式图像压缩在极低比特率下的重建质量，为DIC领域提供了新范式。

Original Abstract: Distributed Image Compression (DIC) is crucial for multi-view transmission, especially when operating at extremely low bitrates (< 0.1 bpp). Its core challenge is effectively utilizing side information to achieve high-quality reconstruction under strict bitrate budgets. However, existing DIC approaches struggle to exploit global context and object-level details from side information, leading to local blurring and the loss of fine details in the reconstruction. To address these limitations, we propose a Multimodal DIC framework (MDIC), which, for the first time, leverages side information in a multimodal manner into the DIC paradigm, effectively preserving fine-grained local details and enhancing global perceptual quality in reconstructed images. Specifically, we introduce a text-to-image diffusion-based decoder conditioned on textual side information extracted from correlated images to capture shared global semantics. Moreover, we design a feature-mask generator, supervised by a multimodal fine-grained alignment task, to strengthen the exploitation of visual side information. The generated mask serves two purposes: first, it guides the extraction of fine-grained details from losslessly transmitted side information to preserve the semantic consistency of reconstructed details; second, it regulates the extraction of clustered feature representations from the quantized VQ-VAE embeddings, compensating for category information lost under the extreme compression of the primary image. Extensive experiments on the widely used KITTI Stereo and Cityscapes datasets demonstrate that MDIC achieves state-of-the-art perceptual quality at extremely low bitrates.

中文摘要翻译: 分布式图像压缩（DIC）对于多视角传输至关重要，特别是在极低比特率（<0.1 bpp）下运行时。其核心挑战在于有效利用侧信息，在严格的比特率预算下实现高质量重建。然而，现有的DIC方法难以从侧信息中利用全局上下文和物体级细节，导致重建中出现局部模糊和细节丢失。为解决这些局限性，我们提出了多模态DIC框架（MDIC），首次以多模态方式将侧信息引入DIC范式，有效保留了重建图像中的细粒度局部细节并增强了全局感知质量。具体而言，我们引入了一个基于文本到图像的扩散解码器，该解码器以从相关图像中提取的文本侧信息为条件，捕获共享的全局语义。此外，我们设计了一个特征掩码生成器，通过多模态细粒度对齐任务进行监督，以增强对视觉侧信息的利用。生成的掩码有两个用途：首先，它指导从无损传输的侧信息中提取细粒度细节，以保持重建细节的语义一致性；其次，它调节从量化VQ-VAE嵌入中提取聚类特征表示，补偿主图像在极端压缩下丢失的类别信息。在广泛使用的KITTI Stereo和Cityscapes数据集上进行的大量实验表明，MDIC在极低比特率下实现了最优的感知质量。

</details>


### [37] [COCOTree: A Dataset and Benchmark for Open Tree-Structured Visual Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22068)
*Junhyub Lee,Seunghun Chae,Hyosu Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了开放树分解（Open Tree Decomposition）任务，并构建了COCOTree大规模基准数据集，结合LVLMs和SAM 3实现全自动标注，同时提出了OTQ评估指标。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图像分割方法缺乏对图像组件层次化树结构的建模能力，传统人工标注存在高昂的认知和物理瓶颈，亟需一种自动化、开放词汇、细粒度可变的层次化图像分解方法。

Method: 1) 设计全自动标注流水线，融合LVLMs的语义推理与SAM 3的精确几何分割；2) 构建COCOTree数据集（21K图像、180万结构节点、3500+开放词汇标签）；3) 提出开放树质量（OTQ）指标，联合评估掩码精度、标签准确性和结构一致性。

Result: COCOTree包含21K+图像和180万结构节点，覆盖3500+开放词汇标签，成功捕捉复杂物理组件的长尾分布；人工评估证实自动生成的标注与人类结构判断高度一致。

Conclusion: 开放树分解任务可行且有效，所提自动化流水线和基准数据集为层次化图像理解提供了新范式，OTQ指标为标准化评估建立了协议。

Original Abstract: We formalize and enable the task of open tree decomposition, which segments an image into hierarchical trees of visual components with unconstrained granularity and flexibility. Specifically, we provide the foundation benchmark for this new paradigm with the following three key contributions. First, we overcome the prohibitively high cognitive and physical bottlenecks of manual annotation by developing a fully automated generation pipeline that synergizes the semantic reasoning of Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) with the precise geometric grounding of SAM 3. Second, leveraging this pipeline, we construct COCOTree, a massive-scale benchmark featuring over 21K images and 1.8M structural nodes. By embracing an open-vocabulary space of over 3.5K unique labels, it successfully captures the long-tail distribution of complex physical assemblies. Notably, rigorous human evaluation confirms our generated annotations demonstrate strong alignment with human structural judgment. Third, we establish a standardized evaluation protocol by proposing the Open Tree Quality (OTQ) metric, which jointly assesses mask precision, label accuracy, and structural consistency. We release our dataset and benchmark code at https://github.com/melonkick3090/COCOTree.

中文摘要翻译: 我们形式化并实现了开放树分解（open tree decomposition）任务，该任务能将图像分割成具有无约束粒度和灵活性的视觉组件层次树。具体而言，我们通过以下三个关键贡献为该新范式提供了基础基准。首先，我们通过开发全自动生成流程，将大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）的语义推理与SAM 3的精确几何定位协同起来，克服了手动标注在认知和物理上的高昂瓶颈。其次，利用该流程，我们构建了COCOTree，一个包含超过21K张图像和180万个结构节点的大规模基准数据集。通过采用超过3500个唯一标签的开放词汇空间，它成功捕捉了复杂物理组件的长尾分布。值得注意的是，严格的人工评估证实我们生成的标注与人类结构判断高度一致。第三，我们提出了开放树质量（OTQ）指标，建立了标准化评估协议，该指标联合评估掩码精度、标签准确性和结构一致性。我们在https://github.com/melonkick3090/COCOTree上发布数据集和基准代码。

</details>


### [38] [TWINGS: Thin Plate Splines Warp-aligned Initialization for Sparse-View Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22069)
*Hyeseong Kim,Geonhui Son,Deukhee Lee,Dosik Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TWINGS通过引入薄板样条（TPS）变形模型对齐反投影点与三角化控制点，为稀疏视图下的3D高斯泼溅提供快速且几何精确的初始化，显著提升重建质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 稀疏视图输入下，现有3D高斯泼溅方法因点云稀疏性难以实现高质量场景重建，需要一种能有效处理点稀疏性的初始化方法。

Method: 采用薄板样条（TPS）作为光滑非刚性变形模型，通过最小化弯曲能量从控制点对应关系估计全局一致性变形，将深度估计的反投影点与三角化3D控制点对齐，生成校准后的反投影点；在控制点附近采样这些校准点，为3DGS提供快速且几何精确的初始化。

Result: 在DTU、LLFF和Mip-NeRF360数据集上的大量实验表明，TWINGS在稀疏视图场景下 consistently 优于现有方法，实现了细节丰富且精确的重建。

Conclusion: TWINGS有效解决了稀疏视图下3DGS的点稀疏性问题，显著提升了结构细节保持和色彩保真度。

Original Abstract: Novel view synthesis from sparse-view inputs poses a significant challenge in 3D computer vision, particularly for achieving high-quality scene reconstructions with limited viewpoints. We introduce TWINGS, a framework that enhances 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) by directly addressing point sparsity. We employ Thin Plate Splines (TPS), a smooth non-rigid deformation model that minimizes bending energy to estimate a globally coherent warp from control-point correspondences, to align backprojected points from estimated depth with triangulated 3D control points, yielding calibrated backprojected points. By sampling these calibrated points near the control points, TWINGS provides a fast and geometrically accurate initialization for 3DGS, ultimately improving structural detail preservation and color fidelity in reconstructed scenes. Extensive experiments on DTU, LLFF, and Mip-NeRF360 demonstrate that TWINGS consistently outperforms existing methods, delivering detailed and accurate reconstructions under sparse-view scenarios.

中文摘要翻译: 稀疏视图输入下的新视角合成是3D计算机视觉中的一个重大挑战，尤其在视角有限的情况下实现高质量场景重建。本文提出TWINGS框架，通过直接应对点稀疏性问题来增强3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）。我们采用薄板样条（TPS）——一种通过最小化弯曲能量来从控制点对应关系估计全局一致性变形的光滑非刚性变形模型——将深度估计的反投影点与三角化3D控制点对齐，从而生成校准后的反投影点。通过在这些控制点附近采样校准点，TWINGS为3DGS提供了快速且几何精确的初始化，最终提升了重建场景中的结构细节保留和色彩保真度。在DTU、LLFF和Mip-NeRF360上的大量实验表明，TWINGS在稀疏视图场景下持续优于现有方法，实现了细节丰富且精确的重建。

</details>


### [39] [JMed48k: A Multi-Profession Japanese Medical Licensing Benchmark for Vision-Language Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22080)
*Yue Xun,Junyu Liu,Qian Niu,Xinyi Wang,Zheng Yuan,Zirui Li,Zequn Zhang,Bowen Zhao,Shujun Wang,Irene Li,Kan Hatakeyama-Sato,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JMed48k是一个包含48,862道日本国家医疗资格考试题目和20,142张图像的多职业视觉语言模型评测基准。通过评估21个模型并引入配对图像移除审计，发现闭源和开源模型从图像中获益显著，而医疗专用模型对视觉证据的利用有限。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有医疗视觉语言模型评测基准多局限于单一职业或英语语境，缺乏对多职业、多模态、日本医疗场景的系统性评估，且难以衡量模型对视觉信息的真实利用程度。

Method: 构建JMed48k数据集（含11种国家资格考试、8类视觉内容标注），从中提取JMed48k-Eval子集（近5年12,484道计分题）；设计配对图像移除审计方法，对比同一问题在有/无图像情况下的答题状态变化，分析四种答案转换状态。

Result: 评估了21个模型（闭源、开源、医疗专用）；闭源和开源模型从图像中获益显著，而医疗专用系统在移除图像后仍能维持许多正确答案；图像移除净效应在不同职业间差异可达7倍（医师+5.7分 vs. 公共卫生护士+39.8分）。

Conclusion: JMed48k为视觉语言模型在医疗资格考试场景中提供了可复现、按职业分层评估的基准，揭示了不同模型家族对多模态信息的利用差异，支持更加细粒度的性能分析。

Original Abstract: We introduce JMed48k, a multi-profession Japanese healthcare licensing benchmark for evaluating vision-language models. Built from official PDF materials released by the Japanese Ministry of Health, Labour and Welfare, JMed48k contains 48,862 exam questions and 20,142 images from 11 national licensing examinations between 2005 and 2025, with visual content annotated under an 8-type taxonomy. From this corpus, we derive JMed48k-Eval, a recent five-year evaluation subset with 12,484 scored questions, including 9,905 text-only questions and 2,579 questions with images. We evaluate 21 proprietary, open-source, and medical-specific models, reporting text-only and with-image performance separately. Because these subsets contain different questions, we further introduce a paired image-removal audit that evaluates questions with images before and after removing visual content to explore four answer-transition states. The audit shows that proprietary and open source models gain substantially from images, whereas medical-specific systems show limited observable use of visual evidence, with many correct answers persisting after image removal. Even among proprietary models, the net image-removal effect varies sevenfold across professions, from +5.7 points on Physician questions to +39.8 points on Public Health Nurse questions. We release JMed48k to support reproducible, profession-stratified evaluation of vision-language models in medical licensing settings.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了JMed48k，一个面向日本多职业医疗资格考试的视觉语言模型评测基准。该基准基于日本厚生劳动省发布的官方PDF材料构建，包含2005年至2025年间11类国家资格考试的48,862道试题和20,142张图像，视觉内容按8类分类体系进行了标注。从该语料库中，我们进一步提取了JMed48k-Eval子集，包含近五年12,484道计分题，其中9,905道为纯文本题，2,579道为带图题。我们对21个闭源、开源及医疗专用模型进行了评估，分别报告了纯文本和带图条件下的性能。由于这些子集包含不同的问题，我们进一步引入了一种配对图像移除审计方法，评测带图问题在移除视觉内容前后的答题情况，以探索四种答案转换状态。审计结果显示，闭源和开源模型从图像中获益显著，而医疗专用系统对视觉证据的利用有限——许多正确答案在图像移除后仍然保持不变。即使在闭源模型中，图像移除的净效应在不同职业间也呈现出高达7倍的差异，从医师问题的+5.7分到公共卫生护士问题的+39.8分。我们发布JMed48k，以支持在医疗资格考试场景中对视觉语言模型进行可复现、按职业分层评估。

</details>


### [40] [GenHAR: Generalizing Cross-domain Human Activity Recognition for Last-mile Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22086)
*Zhiqing Hong,Zelong Li,Xiubin Fan,Guang Yang,Baoshen Guo,Haotian Wang,Tian He,Desheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GenHAR是一个通过学习域不变传感器表示来解决人类活动识别中分布偏移问题的框架，在准确率上提升9.97%，计算量降低6.4倍，并已在4个城市部署检测21.5亿次实时活动。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有HAR方法面临不同传感器数据域之间的分布偏移问题，导致在真实部署时性能下降。

Method: GenHAR包含两个核心创新：(1) 对传感器数据进行令牌化并学习频率传感器通道维度之间的相关性，以增强模型鲁棒性；(2) 通过选择性掩码和高效注意力机制提升计算效率。

Result: 相比最先进方法，GenHAR准确率提升9.97%，浮点运算量减少6.4倍；已在4个城市部署，检测21.5亿次实时活动。

Conclusion: GenHAR通过域不变传感器表示学习有效缓解了HAR中的分布偏移问题，兼顾了高性能与高效率，具备实际部署价值。

Original Abstract: Human Activity Recognition (HAR) has shown remarkable effectiveness in various applications, such as smart healthcare and intelligent manufacturing. However, a major challenge faced by HAR is the distribution shift across different sensor data domains, which often leads to decreased performance when deployed for real-world applications. To address this issue, this paper introduces GenHAR, a novel framework designed to mitigate the domain gap by learning domain-invariant sensor representations. GenHAR aims to enhance the generalization capabilities of HAR on target domains purely with data from the source domain. The key novelty of GenHAR lies in two aspects. Firstly, GenHAR tokenizes sensor data and learns correlations among frequency sensor channel dimensions to improve the robustness of HAR models. Secondly, GenHAR improves the efficiency via selective masking and an efficient attention mechanism. We conduct a systematic analysis of GenHAR by comparing it with state-of-the-art HAR methods on real-world human activity datasets. Results show that GenHAR outperforms state-of-the-art methods by 9.97% in accuracy, and reduces Floating Point Operations by 6.4 times. Moreover, we deploy GenHAR at a leading logistics company in 4 cities, and have detected 2.15 billion real-time activities. We release our code at: https://github.com/Sensor-FoundationModel/GenHAR.

中文摘要翻译: 人类活动识别（HAR）在智能医疗和智能制造等多种应用中展现出了显著的效果。然而，HAR面临的一个主要挑战是不同传感器数据域之间的分布偏移，这通常会导致在真实世界应用部署时性能下降。为了解决这一问题，本文引入了GenHAR，一种通过学习域不变传感器表示来缩小域差距的新型框架。GenHAR旨在仅使用源域数据即可增强HAR在目标域上的泛化能力。GenHAR的关键创新体现在两个方面。首先，GenHAR对传感器数据进行令牌化，并学习频率传感器通道维度之间的相关性，以提高HAR模型的鲁棒性。其次，GenHAR通过选择性掩码和高效注意力机制提升了计算效率。我们通过在真实世界人类活动数据集上与最先进的HAR方法进行系统性对比分析来评估GenHAR。结果表明，GenHAR在准确率上比最先进方法高出9.97%，并将浮点运算量减少了6.4倍。此外，我们在4个城市的一家领先物流公司部署了GenHAR，检测了21.5亿次实时活动。我们在以下地址发布代码：https://github.com/Sensor-FoundationModel/GenHAR。

</details>


### [41] [LVDrive: Latent Visual Representation Enhanced Vision-Language-Action Autonomous Driving Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22089)
*Xiaodong Mei,Diankun Zhang,Hongwei Xie,Guang Chen,Hangjun Ye,Dan Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LVDrive，一种通过潜在视觉表示增强的VLA框架，用于自动驾驶。它在VLA范式中引入未来场景预测任务，在高维潜在空间中学习未来表示，并在单次前向传播中联合建模未来场景和运动预测，最后通过两阶段轨迹解码策略利用学习到的潜在未来表示来优化轨迹生成。在Bench2Drive基准上实现了显著的闭环驾驶性能提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的VLA模型依赖稀疏的动作监督，未能充分利用其强大的场景理解与推理能力；而近期尝试通过世界模型引入密集视觉监督的方法，则过度强调像素级图像重建，忽略了语义上有意义的场景表示学习。

Method: (1) 在未来场景预测任务中，未来表示完全在高维潜在空间中学习，并受到预训练视觉骨干的辅助监督。(2) 摒弃低效的自回归生成，在统一的嵌入空间中联合建模未来场景和运动预测，通过单次前向传播实现未来感知推理。(3) 设计两阶段轨迹解码策略，显式利用学习到的潜在未来表示来优化轨迹生成。

Result: 在具有挑战性的Bench2Drive基准上，LVDrive在闭环驾驶性能上取得了显著提升，优于基于动作监督的方法和基于图像重建的世界模型方法。

Conclusion: LVDrive通过引入潜在空间中的未来场景预测任务，有效增强了VLA框架的场景理解与推理能力，为端到端自动驾驶提供了一种超越稀疏动作监督和像素级重建的新范式。

Original Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising framework for end-to-end autonomous driving. However, existing VLAs typically rely on sparse action supervision, which underutilizes their powerful scene understanding and reasoning capabilities. Recent attempts to incorporate dense visual supervision via world modeling often overemphasize pixel-level image reconstruction, neglecting semantically meaningful scene representation learning. In this work, we propose LVDrive, a Latent Visual representation enhanced VLA framework for autonomous driving. LVDrive introduces a future scene prediction task into the VLA paradigm, where future representations are learned entirely in a high-level latent space under auxiliary supervision from a pretrained vision backbone. Departing from inefficient autoregressive generation, we jointly model future scene and motion prediction within a unified embedding space, processed in a single forward pass to conduct the future-aware reasoning. We further design a two-stage trajectory decoding strategy that explicitly leverages the learned latent future representations to refine trajectory generation. Extensive experiments on the challenging Bench2Drive benchmark demonstrate that LVDrive achieves significant improvements in closed-loop driving performance, outperforming both action supervised methods and image-reconstruction-based world model approaches.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言-动作模型已成为端到端自动驾驶中一种有前景的框架。然而，现有的VLA模型通常依赖稀疏的动作监督，未能充分利用其强大的场景理解与推理能力。近期通过世界模型引入密集视觉监督的尝试，往往过度强调像素级图像重建，忽略了语义上有意义的场景表示学习。本文提出了LVDrive，一种通过潜在视觉表示增强的VLA自动驾驶框架。LVDrive将未来场景预测任务引入VLA范式，其中未来表示完全在高维潜在空间中学习，并受到预训练视觉骨干的辅助监督。与低效的自回归生成不同，我们在统一的嵌入空间中联合建模未来场景和运动预测，通过单次前向传播实现未来感知推理。我们进一步设计了两阶段轨迹解码策略，显式利用学习到的潜在未来表示来优化轨迹生成。在具有挑战性的Bench2Drive基准上的大量实验表明，LVDrive在闭环驾驶性能上取得了显著提升，优于基于动作监督的方法和基于图像重建的世界模型方法。

</details>


### [42] [VISTA: Validation-Guided Integration of Spatial and Temporal Foundation Models with Anatomical Decoding for Rare-Pathology VCE Event Detection -- after competition results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22096)
*Bo-Cheng Qiu,Fang-Ying Lin,Ming-Han Sun,Yu-Fan Lin,Chia-Ming Lee,Chih-Chung Hsu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VISTA是一个面向胶囊内镜事件检测的多骨干框架，结合EndoFM-LV和DINOv3提取时域与视觉特征，通过DHE、VGWF和ATED模块提升事件级检测性能，在RAREVISION任务中取得第二名。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 胶囊内镜事件检测具有临床相关发现稀疏、视觉异质性高、评估以事件级而非帧级准确率为标准等特点，现有方法难以有效建模时域上下文与帧级语义的联合表征。

Method: VISTA框架包含三大模块：1）Diverse Head Ensemble (DHE) 多头集成；2）Validation-Guided Weighted Fusion (VGWF) 验证引导加权融合；3）Anatomy-Aware Temporal Event Decoding (ATED) 解剖感知时域事件解码。结合EndoFM-LV提取时域上下文，DINOv3 ViTL/16提取帧级视觉语义。

Result: 官方提交成绩为隐藏测试集 temporal mAP@0.5 0.3530、mAP@0.95 0.3235；赛后通过全局粗搜索扩展局部阈值精调，提升至mAP@0.5 0.3726和mAP@0.95 0.3431，排名第二。

Conclusion: VISTA框架通过多骨干特征融合与事件级解码策略有效提升了稀疏、异质性高的胶囊内镜事件检测性能，且后处理优化可进一步带来显著提升。

Original Abstract: Capsule endoscopy event detection is challenging because clinically relevant findings are sparse, visually heterogeneous, and evaluated at the event level rather than by frame accuracy. We propose VISTA, a metric-aligned multi-backbone framework for the RAREVISION task. VISTA combines EndoFM-LV for temporal context and DINOv3 ViTL/16 for frame-level visual semantics, followed by a Diverse Head Ensemble (DHE), Validation-Guided Weighted Fusion (VGWF), and Anatomy-Aware Temporal Event Decoding (ATED). The original official submission achieved hidden-test temporal mAP@0.5 of 0.3530 and mAP@0.95 of 0.3235. After the competition, extending local threshold refinement with a global coarse search improved performance to 0.3726 mAP@0.5 and 0.3431 mAP@0.95, ranking Team ACVLab second in the post-competition evaluation.

中文摘要翻译: 胶囊内镜事件检测具有挑战性，因为临床相关发现稀疏、视觉异质性高，且评估以事件级而非帧级准确率为标准。我们提出VISTA，一个面向RAREVISION任务的指标对齐多骨干框架。VISTA结合了用于时域上下文的EndoFM-LV和用于帧级视觉语义的DINOv3 ViTL/16，随后接入多样头集成（DHE）、验证引导加权融合（VGWF）和解剖感知时域事件解码（ATED）。原始官方提交在隐藏测试集上的temporal mAP@0.5为0.3530，mAP@0.95为0.3235。赛后，通过全局粗搜索扩展局部阈值精调，性能提升至mAP@0.5 0.3726和mAP@0.95 0.3431，使Team ACVLab在赛后评估中排名第二。

</details>


### [43] [OPERA: An Agent for Image Restoration with End-to-End Joint Planning-Execution Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22104)
*Feng Zhu,Shuyang Xie,Yihan Zeng,Ming Liu,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OPERA提出了一种端到端联合优化规划与执行的智能体框架，通过强化学习优化工具组合、协同训练恢复工具，显著提升了复杂混合退化下的图像恢复性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于智能体的图像恢复方法受限于隐式约束的规划空间以及预训练工具之间缺乏协调，性能存在根本性局限。

Method: OPERA框架：在规划侧，使用强化学习以最终恢复质量为奖励，在组合规划空间上直接优化工具组合；在执行侧，引入智能体引导的协同训练，使恢复工具在顺序组合下学习协作行为。

Result: 在多退化基准和真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，OPERA在多样且复杂的退化场景中 consistently 优于全能恢复模型和现有基于智能体的方法。

Conclusion: 端到端联合优化规划与执行是提升复杂混合退化图像恢复性能的有效途径。

Original Abstract: Real-world image restoration is challenging due to complex and interacting mixed degradations. Recent agent-based approaches address this problem by composing multiple task-specific restoration tools. However, empirical analysis reveals that their performance is fundamentally limited by implicitly constrained planning spaces and the lack of coordination among independently pretrained tools. To address these issues, we propose OPERA (Optimized Planning-Execution Restoration Agent), a framework that jointly optimizes restoration planning and tool execution in an end-to-end manner. On the planning side, OPERA uses reinforcement learning to directly optimize tool composition over a combinatorial plan space, with the final restoration quality as the reward. On the execution side, OPERA introduces agent-guided co-training of restoration tools, enabling them to learn cooperative behaviors under sequential composition. Extensive experiments on multi-degradation benchmarks and real-world datasets demonstrate that OPERA consistently outperforms both all-in-one restoration models and existing agent-based methods across diverse and complex degradation scenarios.

中文摘要翻译: 真实世界的图像恢复由于复杂且相互作用的混合退化而极具挑战性。最近的基于智能体的方法通过组合多个任务特定的恢复工具来应对这一问题。然而，经验分析表明，它们的性能根本性地受限于隐式约束的规划空间以及独立预训练工具之间缺乏协调。为解决这些问题，我们提出OPERA（优化规划-执行恢复智能体），一个以端到端方式联合优化恢复规划与工具执行的框架。在规划侧，OPERA使用强化学习以最终恢复质量为奖励，在组合规划空间上直接优化工具组合。在执行侧，OPERA引入智能体引导的恢复工具协同训练，使它们能够在顺序组合下学习协作行为。在多退化基准和真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，OPERA在多样且复杂的退化场景中 consistently 优于全能恢复模型和现有的基于智能体的方法。

</details>


### [44] [MotionDPS: Motion-Compensated 3D Brain MRI Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22121)
*Antonio Ortiz-Gonzalez,Erich Kobler,Lukas Schletter,Alexander Effland*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种统一的贝叶斯框架，利用预训练的3D复值分数扩散模型作为解剖图像先验，联合估计刚体运动参数、线圈灵敏度图和解剖图像，实现无监督的运动补偿3D MRI重建。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: MRI对患者运动高度敏感，轻微的运动会引入相位不一致，导致模糊、重影和几何畸变等伪影，严重影响诊断质量。现有的回顾性运动补偿方法在加速采集中面临联合重建与运动估计的病态问题，难以获得高质量的重建结果。

Method: 提出统一的贝叶斯框架，将预训练的3D复值基于分数的扩散模型作为解剖图像先验，结合基于物理的前向模型。推理时交替进行扩散后验图像更新和针对运动及线圈灵敏度估计的邻近优化步骤，实现完全无监督的重建，无需配对的运动-free训练数据。

Result: 在模拟和真实运动脑MRI数据集上的实验表明，与最先进的经典和基于学习的运动校正方法相比，该方法在严重运动和高速率加速条件下均获得了更好的图像质量和运动鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一贝叶斯框架能够有效解决运动补偿3D MRI中的联合重建与运动估计问题，特别是在严重运动和高速加速场景下表现优异，且无需配对的洁净训练数据。

Original Abstract: Magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is highly susceptible to patient motion due to its relatively long acquisition times and the fact that data are acquired sequentially in k-space. Even small patient movements introduce phase inconsistencies across measurements, leading to severe artifacts such as blurring, ghosting, and geometric distortions that can compromise diagnostic quality. Retrospective motion compensation remains challenging, particularly in accelerated acquisitions, due to the ill-posed nature of the joint reconstruction and motion estimation problem. In this work, we propose a unified Bayesian framework for motion-compensated 3D MRI that jointly estimates the anatomical image, rigid-body motion parameters, and coil sensitivity maps directly from motion-corrupted k-space data. Our approach integrates pretrained 3D complex-valued score-based diffusion models as expressive anatomical image priors within a physics-based forward model. Inference is performed by alternating diffusion posterior image updates with efficient proximal optimization steps for motion and coil sensitivity estimation, enabling fully unsupervised reconstruction without the need for paired motion-free training data. Experiments on simulated and real-motion brain MRI datasets demonstrate that the proposed method achieves improved image quality and motion robustness compared to state-of-the-art classical and learning-based motion correction techniques, particularly in the presence of severe motion and high acceleration.

中文摘要翻译: 磁共振成像（MRI）由于其相对较长的采集时间以及数据在k空间中顺序采集的特性，对患者运动高度敏感。即使是微小的患者运动也会在测量之间引入相位不一致，导致模糊、重影和几何畸变等严重伪影，从而影响诊断质量。由于联合重建与运动估计问题本身的病态性，回顾性运动补偿仍然具有挑战性，尤其是在加速采集中。在本工作中，我们提出了一种统一的贝叶斯框架，用于运动补偿的3D MRI，该框架直接从运动损坏的k空间数据中联合估计解剖图像、刚体运动参数和线圈灵敏度图。我们的方法将预训练的3D复值基于分数的扩散模型作为表达性解剖图像先验，集成在基于物理的前向模型中。推理过程通过交替进行扩散后验图像更新与针对运动和线圈灵敏度估计的高效邻近优化步骤来实现，使得完全无监督的重建成为可能，无需配对的运动-free训练数据。在模拟和真实运动脑MRI数据集上的实验表明，与最先进的经典和基于学习的运动校正技术相比，所提出的方法在图像质量和运动鲁棒性方面均有提升，特别是在严重运动和高速率加速条件下表现尤为突出。

</details>


### [45] [AesFormer: Transform Everyday Photos into Beautiful Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22126)
*Tianxiang Du,Hulingxiao He,Yuxin Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AesFormer两阶段框架，通过解耦美学规划与图像编辑，实现照片的结构性美学重构，并构建了AesRecon基准数据集，在APR任务上取得显著提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有修图和人像增强方法无法修复照片中的结构性缺陷（如构图、视角、姿态），而近年图像编辑模型虽能实现编辑但缺乏美学理解，生成的编辑结果语义合理但美学效果弱。

Method: 提出AesFormer两阶段框架：阶段1通过AesThinker美学动作模型沿七个渐进式摄影维度分析输入图像并输出可执行编辑动作，并应用GRPO-A鼓励多样化动作探索；阶段2由AesEditor根据动作引导执行结构性编辑。此外构建视频语料挖掘管道VCMP和AesRecon基准数据集（9,071对严格对齐的好坏图像对）。

Result: 实验表明AesFormer显著提升了APR性能，且与Nano Banana Pro相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: AesFormer通过将美学规划与图像编辑解耦，有效解决了照片结构性美学重构问题，验证了分阶段策略在美学增强中的有效性。

Original Abstract: In everyday photography, aesthetically appealing moments are often captured with structural flaws (e.g., composition, camera viewpoint, or pose) that existing retouching and portrait enhancement methods cannot fix. We formulate Aesthetic Photo Reconstruction (APR) as improving a photo's aesthetic quality via structural reconstruction while preserving subject identity and scene semantics. Although recent advances in image editing models make APR feasible, they often lack aesthetic understanding, yielding edits that are semantically plausible yet aesthetically weak. To address this, we propose AesFormer, a two-stage framework that decouples aesthetic planning from image editing. In Stage 1, an aesthetic action model (AesThinker) analyzes the input along seven progressive photographic dimensions and outputs executable editing actions; we further apply GRPO-A to encourage broad exploration over diverse action plans beyond SFT. In Stage 2, an action-conditioned editor (AesEditor) performs structural edits guided by these actions. To support APR, we build a video-based corpus-mining pipeline (VCMP) and construct AesRecon, a benchmark of 9,071 strictly aligned (poor, good) image pairs. Experiments show that AesFormer substantially improves APR performance and is competitive with Nano Banana Pro. Code is available at https://github.com/PKU-ICST-MIPL/AesFormer_ICML2026.

中文摘要翻译: 在日常生活中，具有美感的瞬间往往伴随着结构性缺陷（如构图、相机视角或姿态），现有修图和人像增强方法无法修复这些问题。本文将美学照片重构（APR）定义为一类通过结构性重构提升照片美学质量，同时保持主体身份和场景语义的任务。尽管近期图像编辑模型的进展使APR变得可行，但这些模型通常缺乏美学理解能力，生成的编辑结果在语义上合理但美学效果薄弱。为此，我们提出AesFormer，一个将美学规划与图像编辑解耦的两阶段框架。在阶段1中，美学动作模型（AesThinker）沿七个渐进式摄影维度分析输入图像，并输出可执行的编辑动作；我们进一步应用GRPO-A以鼓励在监督微调（SFT）之外探索更多样化的动作方案。在阶段2中，动作条件编辑器（AesEditor）在这些动作的引导下执行结构性编辑。为支持APR任务，我们构建了一个基于视频的语料挖掘管道（VCMP），并构建了AesRecon基准数据集，包含9,071对严格对齐的（劣质、优质）图像对。实验表明，AesFormer显著提升了APR性能，且与Nano Banana Pro相比具有竞争力。代码已开源：https://github.com/PKU-ICST-MIPL/AesFormer_ICML2026。

</details>


### [46] [EventGait: Towards Robust Gait Recognition with Event Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22139)
*Senyan Xu,Shuai Chen,Chuanfu Shen,Kean Liu,Zhijing Sun,Chengzhi Cao,Xueyang Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EventGait，一个基于事件相机的端到端双流步态识别框架，通过混合脉冲专家（MoSE）建模动态运动、跨模态结构对齐（CroSA）学习静态形状，在正常和低光条件下均达到SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统相机在非受控环境下受光照和运动灵敏度影响，而事件相机虽有微秒级时间分辨率和宽动态范围优势，但现有事件方法将事件流聚合为事件图像，丢失了步态识别所需的细粒度运动动态信息。

Method: 提出EventGait端到端双流框架：动态流使用混合脉冲专家（MoSE），引入多种神经元时间常数以鲁棒感知复杂运动和光照场景下的动态特征；静态流通过跨模态结构对齐（CroSA）与大视觉基础模型对齐，学习密集形状表征。

Result: 在合成数据集SUSTech1K-E和真实数据集CCGR-Mini-E上均达到SOTA；正常条件下与基于相机的步态识别性能相当，在低光场景中显著超越；同时发布了两个新的大规模事件步态基准数据集。

Conclusion: 事件相机驱动的步态分析具有鲁棒性和巨大潜力，EventGait为事件步态识别设立了新标杆，代码和数据集已开源。

Original Abstract: Gait recognition enables non-intrusive, privacy-preserving identification but suffers in uncontrolled environments due to illumination and motion sensitivity of conventional cameras. In this work, we explore gait recognition using event cameras, which offer microsecond temporal resolution and high dynamic range, naturally capturing robust dynamic cues and suppressing static noise. Existing event-based approaches typically aggregate event streams into event images over long time windows, thereby discarding fine-grained motion dynamics critical for gait recognition. Therefore, we propose \textbf{EventGait}, an end-to-end dual-stream framework that separately models motion and shape while preserving the advantages of events. Our dynamic stream leverages a Mixture of Spiking Experts (MoSE) with diverse neuron constants for robust dynamic perception across complex motion and illumination scenes, while the static stream learns dense shape representations via Cross-modal Structure Alignment (CroSA) with large vision foundation models. To address the absence of large-scale event-based gait datasets, we introduce a synthesis pipeline and release two new benchmarks: SUSTech1K-E and CCGR-Mini-E. Extensive experiments have shown that event-based gait recognition not only achieves results comparable to camera-based gait recognition under normal conditions but also significantly outperforms it in low-light scenarios. Our approach sets a new state of the art on both synthesized and real-world event-based gait benchmarks, highlighting the robustness and potential of event-driven gait analysis. The code and datasets are released at https://github.com/QUEAHREN/EventGait.

中文摘要翻译: 步态识别能够实现非侵入性、保护隐私的身份识别，但在非受控环境中由于传统相机对光照和运动的敏感性而表现不佳。在这项工作中，我们探索了使用事件相机的步态识别，该类相机具有微秒级时间分辨率和高动态范围，能自然捕获鲁棒的动态线索并抑制静态噪声。现有基于事件的方法通常将事件流在长时间窗口内聚合为事件图像，从而丢弃了对步态识别至关重要的细粒度运动动态信息。因此，我们提出了EventGait——一个端到端的双流框架，在保留事件优势的同时分别建模运动和形状。我们的动态流采用具有多样化神经元时间常数的混合脉冲专家（Mixture of Spiking Experts, MoSE），以在复杂运动和光照场景中实现鲁棒的动态感知；静态流则通过跨模态结构对齐（Cross-modal Structure Alignment, CroSA）与大规模视觉基础模型对齐，学习稠密的形状表征。为解决缺乏大规模事件步态数据集的问题，我们引入了一套合成管道，并发布了两个新的基准数据集：SUSTech1K-E和CCGR-Mini-E。大量实验表明，基于事件相机的步态识别不仅在正常条件下达到了与基于相机的步态识别相当的结果，而且在低光场景中显著优于后者。我们的方法在合成和真实世界的事件步态基准上都取得了新的最优结果，突显了事件驱动步态分析的鲁棒性和潜力。代码和数据集已在https://github.com/QUEAHREN/EventGait开源。

</details>


### [47] [One Sentence, One Drama: Personalized Short-Form Drama Generation via Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22144)
*Yufei Shi,Weilong Yan,Naixuan Huang,Yucheng Chen,Chenyu Zhang,Tao He,Si Yong Yeo,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种层次化多智能体框架“一句一剧”，将用户单句创意转化为完整短剧，通过结构化中间模块和迭代优化，解决了现有方法在叙事节奏、空间一致性和制作质量控制上的不足。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有数字短剧制作依赖单次LLM生成的脚本和松散耦合的流水线，无法满足短剧生成的三个关键要求：叙事节奏（钩子弱、升级不足、结尾无吸引力）、空间一致性（场景布局漂移、角色位置跨片段不一致）以及制作级质量控制（需要大量人工审核修正）。

Method: 提出“一句一剧”层次化多智能体框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于多智能体辩论的故事生成模块，强制短剧节奏和叙事连贯性；(2) 基于3D场景的首帧生成机制，建立共享空间参考以实现跨片段角色位置和场景布局一致性；(3) 多阶段审校循环，在脚本、视觉和视频生成阶段进行全面错误检测和定向修正。此外还引入场景级BGM匹配和场景转换规划以提升沉浸体验。

Result: 在提出的Short-Drama-Bench基准上，该方法在叙事质量、跨片段一致性和整体观看体验上显著优于现有流水线。

Conclusion: 层次化多智能体框架结合结构化中间模块与迭代优化，能够有效提升短剧生成在叙事节奏、空间一致性和制作质量方面的表现，为数字短剧自动化生产提供了可行的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Existing approaches for digital short-drama production typically rely on one-shot LLM generated scripts and loosely coupled pipelines, which fail to satisfy three key requirements of short-drama generation: (1) narrative pacing, resulting in weak hooks, insufficient escalation, and unattractive endings; (2) spatial consistency, leading to drifting scene layouts and inconsistent character positions across clips; and (3) production-level quality control, requiring extensive manual review and correction across script and visual stages. We present One Sentence, One Drama, a hierarchical multi-agent framework that transforms a user's single-sentence idea into a fully produced short drama through structured intermediate modules and iterative refinement. Our approach is built upon three key components: (1) a multi-agent debate-based story generation module that enforces short-drama pacing and narrative coherence; (2) a 3D-grounded first-frame generation mechanism that establishes a shared spatial reference for consistent character positioning and scene layout across clips; and (3) multi-stage reviewer loops that perform comprehensive error detection and targeted revision across script, visual, and video generation stages. We also introduce scene-level BGM matching and scene transition planning to improve the audience's immersive experience. To systematically evaluate this task, we introduce Short-Drama-Bench, a benchmark that extends standard video quality metrics with short-drama-specific criteria. Experimental results demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms existing pipelines in narrative quality, cross-clip consistency, and overall viewing experience.

中文摘要翻译: 现有的数字短剧制作方法通常依赖单次大语言模型生成的脚本和松散耦合的流水线，无法满足短剧生成的三个关键要求：(1) 叙事节奏——导致钩子薄弱、剧情升级不足、结尾缺乏吸引力；(2) 空间一致性——造成场景布局漂移和角色位置跨片段不一致；(3) 制作级质量控制——需要在剧本和视觉阶段进行大量人工审核与修正。本文提出“一句一剧”（One Sentence, One Drama），一个层次化多智能体框架，通过结构化中间模块和迭代优化，将用户的单句创意转化为一部完整的短剧。我们的方法基于三个核心组件：(1) 基于多智能体辩论的故事生成模块，强制执行短剧的节奏和叙事连贯性；(2) 基于3D场景的首帧生成机制，建立共享空间参考，确保跨片段的角色位置和场景布局一致；(3) 多阶段审校循环，在剧本、视觉和视频生成各阶段进行全面错误检测和定向修正。我们还引入场景级背景音乐匹配和场景转换规划，以提升观众的沉浸式体验。为系统评估该任务，我们提出Short-Drama-Bench基准，在标准视频质量指标基础上扩展了短剧特有评价标准。实验结果表明，我们的方法在叙事质量、跨片段一致性和整体观看体验上显著优于现有流水线。

</details>


### [48] [Flow-based Gaussian Splatting for Continuous-Scale Remote Sensing Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22147)
*Jiangwei Mo,Xi Lu,Hanlin Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowGS提出了一种基于流匹配（Flow Matching）和二维高斯溅射（2D Gaussian Splatting）的生成式超分辨率框架，用于遥感图像的任意尺度超分辨率重建，在保证感知质量的同时显著提升了推理效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的超分辨率方法推理步骤多（40-1000步）、推理速度慢，且在连续尺度超分辨率设置下灵活性有限。

Method: FlowGS通过流匹配（Flow Matching）结合快捷一致性约束（shortcut consistency），从噪声到细节先验学习连续概率流，降低生成复杂度；同时采用二维高斯溅射（2D Gaussian Splatting）构建连续特征场，支持任意查询位置的灵活重建。

Result: 在连续尺度和固定尺度超分辨率设置下，FlowGS取得了与现有方法相当的感知质量，同时推理效率大幅提升。

Conclusion: FlowGS通过流匹配和二维高斯溅射有效解决了扩散模型推理慢和连续尺度灵活性不足的问题，为遥感图像任意尺度超分辨率提供了一种高效且高质量的生成式重建方案。

Original Abstract: High-resolution remote sensing images (RSIs) are crucial for Earth observation applications, yet acquiring them is often limited by sensor constraints and costs. In recent years, generative super-resolution (SR) methods, particularly diffusion models, have made significant progress. However, they typically require slow iterative inference with 40--1000 steps and exhibit limited flexibility in continuous-scale SR settings. To address these issues, we propose FlowGS, a generative reconstruction framework for arbitrary-scale SR of RSIs. FlowGS models the high-frequency detail representations between high- and low-resolution images and learns a continuous probability flow from noise to detail priors via flow matching (FM) constrained by shortcut consistency, thereby reducing generative complexity and improving inference efficiency. Additionally, we employ 2D Gaussian splatting to construct a continuous feature field, thereby enabling flexible reconstruction at arbitrary query locations. Experimental results show that FlowGS delivers competitive perceptual quality compared with existing methods in both continuous-scale and fixed-scale SR settings, with substantially improved inference efficiency.

中文摘要翻译: 高分辨率遥感图像对于地球观测应用至关重要，然而受限于传感器硬件和成本，获取高分辨率图像往往存在困难。近年来，生成式超分辨率方法，尤其是扩散模型，取得了显著进展。然而，这些方法通常需要40-1000步的缓慢迭代推理，并且在连续尺度超分辨率设置下灵活性有限。为解决这些问题，我们提出了FlowGS，一种用于遥感图像任意尺度超分辨率的生成式重建框架。FlowGS对高低分辨率图像之间的高频细节表示进行建模，并通过受快捷一致性约束的流匹配，学习从噪声到细节先验的连续概率流，从而降低生成复杂度并提升推理效率。此外，我们采用二维高斯溅射构建连续特征场，从而支持在任意查询位置进行灵活重建。实验结果表明，FlowGS在连续尺度和固定尺度超分辨率设置下均能与现有方法竞争感知质量，同时显著提升了推理效率。

</details>


### [49] [Enhancing Multimodal Large Language Models for Safety-Critical Driving Video Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22185)
*Tomaso Trinci,Henrique Piñeiro Monteagudo,Leonardo Taccari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种融合降采样视频帧、高频遥测数据（IMU/GPS）和专用视觉模型语义信息的多模态管道，以生成高质量伪标签，微调开源MLLM（QwenVL-2.5 + DoRA），在少于50M可训练参数下显著提升对驾驶中安全关键事件（SCE）的识别与解释能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型在通用视觉理解上表现优异，但在安全关键的驾驶场景（如碰撞或近碰撞事件）中，由于缺乏对罕见高动态事件的准确感知与推理能力，应用受限。

Method: 提出一个管道：（1）融合降采样视频帧与高频遥测数据（IMU、GPS）以及专用CV模型的语义信息；（2）生成高质量伪标签（描述性标题和问答对）；（3）基于DoRA适配器对开源QwenVL-2.5模型进行微调。

Result: 在少于50M可训练参数和有限计算预算下，模型在识别和解释安全关键事件方面取得了显著提升。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效增强MLLM在安全关键驾驶场景中的感知与推理能力，且参数量少、计算开销低，具有实际部署潜力。

Original Abstract: Recent advancements in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities in general visual understanding. However, their application to safety-critical driving scenarios remains limited by an inability to accurately perceive and reason about rare high-stakes dynamic events, such as collisions or near-collisions. To address this, we introduce a pipeline that enhances MLLM perception by fusing downsampled video frames with synchronized high-frequency telematics data (IMU and GPS) and semantic insights from specialized computer vision models. Our pipeline generates high-quality pseudo-labels, including descriptive captions and question-answer pairs, specifically designed to train MLLMs to identify and describe Safety-Critical Events (SCEs) in real-world driving footage. We show the effectiveness of our approach fine-tuning the open-source QwenVL-2.5 model via DoRA adapters: our experiments demonstrate significant improvements in identifying and explaining safety-critical events, with fewer than 50M trainable parameters and limited computational budget.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型的最新进展在通用视觉理解方面展现了令人印象深刻的能力。然而，它们在安全关键的驾驶场景中的应用仍然受到限制，原因在于无法准确感知和推理罕见的高风险动态事件（例如碰撞或近碰撞）。为此，我们引入了一种管道，通过融合降采样视频帧与同步的高频遥测数据（IMU和GPS）以及来自专用计算机视觉模型的语义信息，来增强MLLM的感知能力。我们的管道生成高质量的伪标签，包括描述性标题和问答对，专门用于训练MLLM识别和描述真实驾驶视频中的安全关键事件。我们展示了该方法的有效性——通过DoRA适配器对开源QwenVL-2.5模型进行微调：实验结果表明，在少于50M可训练参数且计算预算有限的情况下，模型在识别和解释安全关键事件方面取得了显著提升。

</details>


### [50] [Event-Illumination Collaborative Low-light Image Enhancement with a High-resolution Real-world Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22186)
*Senyan Xu,Zhijing Sun,Kean Liu,Xin Lu,Ruixuan Jiang,Mingyang Huang,Xueyang Fu,Zheng-Jun Zha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种事件-光照协同的低光图像增强框架EIC-LIE，通过事件-光照协同交互模块、光照感知事件滤波器以及首个高分辨率真实世界事件-图像对数据集，在五个数据集上达到SOTA性能，PSNR提升最高1.24dB。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于事件的低光图像增强方法主要关注利用事件的高动态范围信息，但忽略了图像中必要的全局光照信息以及事件信号在真实场景中固有的噪声敏感性。

Method: 1) 提出事件-光照协同交互(EICI)模块，包含前向聚集（聚集不同光照条件下的HDR特征）和反向注入（为光照和事件表示提供互补内容）两个关键过程；2) 提出光照感知事件滤波器(IAEF)，基于图像亮度统计动态降低事件噪声；3) 搭建基于分束器的混合成像系统，构建首个高分辨率真实世界事件-图像对数据集。

Result: 在五个真实世界和合成数据集上，EIC-LIE优于所有现有方法，PSNR最高提升1.24dB，SSIM最高提升0.069。

Conclusion: 事件-光照协同机制能有效结合HDR信息与全局光照信息，同时解决事件噪声问题，显著提升低光图像增强性能。

Original Abstract: Event-based low-light image enhancement (LIE) methods mainly focus on incorporating high dynamic range (HDR) information from events while overlooking the essential global illumination in images and the inherent noise sensitivity of event signals in real-world scenarios. To address these issues, we propose EIC-LIE, an event-illumination collaborative LIE framework. Concretely, we first design an Event-Illumination Collaborative Interaction (EICI) module, which contains two key processes: forward gathering, which gathers HDR features across varying lighting conditions, and backward injection, which provides complementary content for illumination and event representations. Next, we introduce an Illumination-aware Event Filter (IAEF) that dynamically reduces event noise based on brightness statistics derived from images. Additionally, we build a beam-splitter-based hybrid imaging system to collect high-quality event-image pairs with temporal synchronization from dynamic scenes, providing the first high-resolution, real-world event-based LIE dataset. Extensive experiments show that our EIC-LIE outperforms state-of-the-art methods on five real-world and synthetic datasets, significantly surpassing previous methods with improvements of up to 1.24dB in PSNR and 0.069 in SSIM. The code and dataset are released at https://github.com/QUEAHREN/EIC-LIE.

中文摘要翻译: 基于事件的低光图像增强方法主要关注融入事件的高动态范围信息，却忽略了图像中必要的全局光照信息以及事件信号在真实场景中固有的噪声敏感性。为解决这些问题，我们提出了EIC-LIE，一个事件-光照协同的低光图像增强框架。具体而言，我们首先设计了一个事件-光照协同交互模块，包含两个关键过程：前向聚集，即聚集不同光照条件下的高动态范围特征；以及反向注入，即为光照和事件表示提供互补内容。其次，我们引入了一个光照感知事件滤波器，该滤波器基于图像亮度统计信息动态降低事件噪声。此外，我们搭建了一个基于分束器的混合成像系统，用于从动态场景中收集时间同步的高质量事件-图像对，提供了首个高分辨率、真实世界场景的基于事件的低光图像增强数据集。大量实验表明，我们的EIC-LIE在五个真实世界和合成数据集上优于现有最先进方法，PSNR最高提升1.24dB，SSIM最高提升0.069。代码和数据集已发布在https://github.com/QUEAHREN/EIC-LIE。

</details>


### [51] [Ultra-High-Definition Image Quality Assessment via Graph Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22192)
*Shaode Yu,Enqi Chen,Ming Huang,Xuemin Ren,Songnan Zhao,Zhicheng Zhang,Qiurui Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UHD-GCN-BIQA框架，通过图表示学习显式建模UHD图像采样块之间的结构依赖关系，实现高效的超高清图像无参考质量评估，在UHD-IQA基准上取得最低RMSE。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 超高清(UHD)图像的无参考质量评估(BIQA)面临挑战：原生分辨率推理计算成本高，而激进的下采样或孤立裁剪会抑制尺度敏感失真、削弱局部伪影与全局场景上下文的关系。现有方法将采样区域视为独立视图，缺乏对区域间结构依赖关系的显式建模。

Method: 提出UHD-GCN-BIQA框架：(1) 从每张UHD图像中采样宽高比对齐的图像块并编码为图节点；(2) 通过空间邻近性和特征相似性构建混合k近邻图；(3) 采用残差图卷积在区域间传播上下文信息；(4) 门控注意力池化将块级证据聚合成图像级质量预测；(5) 使用指数移动平均归一化多目标损失函数联合优化回归、相关性和排序目标。

Result: 在UHD-IQA基准上，PLCC=0.7784，SRCC=0.8019，RMSE=0.0519，在对比方法中获得最具竞争力的相关性性能以及最低的RMSE。

Conclusion: 基于图的区域关系建模对UHD图像质量评估有效，特别是在改善高分辨率视觉内容下的绝对质量分数估计方面表现优异。

Original Abstract: Blind image quality assessment (BIQA) for ultrahighdefinition (UHD) images remains challenging because native-resolution inference is computationally expensive, whereas aggressive resizing or isolated cropping may suppress scale-sensitive distortions and weaken the relationship between local artifacts and global scene context. This paper aims to improve UHD-BIQA by explicitly modeling the structural dependencies among sampled image regions rather than treating them as independent views, and a graph representation learning framework UHD-GCN-BIQA is proposed. The framework samples aspect-ratio-aligned patches from each UHD image, encodes them as graph nodes, and constructs a hybrid k-nearest-neighbor graph using spatial proximity and feature similarity. Residual graph convolution is used to propagate contextual information across regions, and gated attention pooling aggregates patchlevel evidence into an imagelevel quality prediction. An exponential moving average normalized multiobjective loss function is adopted to stabilize the joint optimization of regression, correlation, and ranking objectives. Experiments on the UHD-IQA benchmark show that UHD-GCN-BIQA achieves PLCC = 0.7784, SRCC = 0.8019, and RMSE = 0.0519, obtaining competitive correlation performance and the lowest RMSE among the compared methods. These results indicate that graph-based region relation modeling is effective for UHD image quality assessment, particularly for improving absolute quality score estimation under high-resolution visual content.

中文摘要翻译: 超高清（UHD）图像的无参考图像质量评估（BIQA）仍具挑战性，因为原生分辨率推理计算成本高昂，而激进的下采样或孤立裁剪可能会抑制对尺度敏感的失真，并削弱局部伪影与全局场景上下文之间的关系。本文旨在通过显式建模采样图像区域之间的结构依赖关系（而非将其视为独立视图）来改进UHD-BIQA，并提出了一种图表示学习框架UHD-GCN-BIQA。该框架从每张UHD图像中采样宽高比对齐的图像块，将其编码为图节点，并利用空间邻近性和特征相似性构建混合k近邻图。采用残差图卷积在区域间传播上下文信息，门控注意力池化将块级证据聚合成图像级质量预测。采用指数移动平均归一化多目标损失函数来稳定回归、相关性和排序目标的联合优化。在UHD-IQA基准上的实验表明，UHD-GCN-BIQA取得了PLCC=0.7784、SRCC=0.8019和RMSE=0.0519的结果，在对比方法中获得了最具竞争力的相关性性能以及最低的RMSE。这些结果表明基于图的区域关系建模对于UHD图像质量评估是有效的，特别是在改善高分辨率视觉内容下的绝对质量分数估计方面。

</details>


### [52] [OSS: Open Suturing Skills Vision-Based Assessment Challenge 2024-2025](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22200)
*Hanna Hoffmann,Setareh Bady,Claas de Boer,Max Kirchner,Jan Egger,Rainer Röhrig,Frank Hölzle,Lennart Johannes Gruber,Kunpeng Xie,Marlon Neuhaus,Victor Alves,Guilherme Barbosa,Leonardo Barroso,João Carvalho,Hao Chen,Gabriella d'Albenzio,André Ferreira,Nuno Gomes,Yuichiro Hayashi,Kousuke Hirasawa,Rebecca Hisey,Seungjae Hong,Seoi Jeong,Tiago Jesus,Daehong Kang,Satoshi Kasai,Shunsuke Kikuchi,Takayuki Kitasaka,Satoshi Kondo,Hyoun-Joong Kong,Youngbin Kong,Atsushi Kouno,Shlomi Laufer,Kyu Eun Lee,Bining Long,Nooshin Maghsoodi,Hiroki Matsuzaki,Evangelos Mazomenos,Ori Meiraz,Kensaku Mori,Marina Music,Masahiro Oda,Roi Papo,Jieun Park,Rafael Piexoto,Saeid Rezaei,Mariana Ribeiro,Soyeon Shin,Yang Shu,Idan Smoller,Danail Stoyanov,Yihui Wang,Xinkai Zhao,Sebastian Bodenstedt,Isabel Funke,Stefanie Speidel,Behrus Hinrichs-Puladi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过MICCAI挑战赛，系统评估了基于视频的开放手术技能评估方法。研究发现通用时空视频模型表现最佳，但细粒度OSATS评分预测仍具挑战，关键点跟踪因遮挡问题效果有限。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管机器学习方法在微创手术技能评估中日益流行，但开放手术领域的应用仍然有限。为了推进开放手术中基于视觉的技能评估，作者组织了专门的MICCAI挑战赛，以基准测试和推动该领域发展。

Method: 组织MICCAI OSS Challenge挑战赛，包含两个年度赛程和三个独立任务：(1)技能水平四分类，(2)完整OSATS八类别评分预测，(3)手部和手术工具跟踪。参赛者提交了深度学习视频模型、跟踪驱动方法和混合方法等多种方案。

Result: 通用时空视频模型持续取得最强性能，概念多样的方法在良好执行下也能达到竞争水平。细粒度OSATS评分预测仍具挑战，但增加训练数据能显著提升效果。关键点跟踪因频繁遮挡和出框问题效果有限，限制了基于运动的技能分析应用。

Conclusion: 本研究基准测试了多种创新方案用于开放手术技能评估，揭示了视频评估方法的潜力和当前局限，为推进自动化技能评估走向临床影响指明了关键方向。

Original Abstract: Achieving high levels of surgical skill through effective training is essential for optimal patient outcomes. Automated, data-driven skill assessment holds significant potential to improve surgical training. While machine learning-based methods are increasingly popular for assessing skills in minimally invasive surgery, their application to open surgery remains limited. We present the results of a dedicated MICCAI challenge designed to benchmark and advance vision-based skill assessment in open surgery.
  The challenge dataset comprises videos of an open suturing training task recorded with a static GoPro camera in a dry-lab setting, with instrument trajectories available in addition to the primary video modality. The OSS Challenge was hosted over two consecutive years, comprising two and three independent tasks, respectively: (1) classifying skill level into four classes, (2) predicting the full Objective Structured Assessment of Technical Skills across eight categories, and (3) tracking hands and surgical tools. Participants submitted diverse solutions including deep learning-based video models, tracking-driven methods, and hybrid approaches.
  General-purpose spatiotemporal video models consistently achieved the strongest performance, though conceptually diverse approaches reached competitive levels when well-executed. Predicting fine-grained OSATS scores remains challenging but benefits substantially from increased training data. Keypoint tracking proves difficult given frequent occlusions and out-of-frame instances, limiting current applicability for motion-based skill analysis. This work benchmarks innovative and diverse solutions for surgical skill assessment, highlighting both the promise and current limitations of video-based evaluation in open surgery and identifying critical directions for advancing automated skill assessment toward clinical impact.

中文摘要翻译: 通过有效培训达到高水平的 surgical 技能对于优化患者治疗效果至关重要。自动化、数据驱动的技能评估在改善手术培训方面具有显著潜力。虽然基于机器学习的方法在微创手术技能评估中日益流行，但其在开放手术中的应用仍然有限。我们展示了一项专门的MICCAI挑战赛的结果，该挑战赛旨在基准测试和推进开放手术中基于视觉的技能评估。
挑战数据集包含在一个干燥实验室环境中使用静态GoPro相机记录的开放缝合训练任务的视频，除了主要视频模态外，还提供器械轨迹数据。OSS Challenge连续举办了两年，分别包含两个和三个独立任务：(1)技能水平四分类，(2)预测完整的外科技能客观结构化评估（OSATS）八大类别评分，(3)手部和手术工具的跟踪。参赛者提交了多种解决方案，包括基于深度学习的视频模型、跟踪驱动方法和混合方法。
通用时空视频模型持续取得了最强性能，尽管概念上多样的方法在良好执行时也能达到竞争水平。预测细粒度OSATS评分仍然具有挑战性，但增加训练数据能显著提升效果。关键点跟踪因频繁的遮挡和画面外实例而困难重重，限制了其在基于运动的技能分析中的当前适用性。本研究基准测试了用于外科技能评估的创新且多样化的解决方案，突出了视频评估在开放手术中的潜力和当前局限，并确定了推动自动化技能评估走向临床影响的关键方向。

</details>


### [53] [Zero-Shot Temporal Action Localization Through Textual Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22201)
*Benedetta Liberatori,Alessandro Conti,Lorenzo Vaquero,Paolo Rota,Yiming Wang,Elisa Ricci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TEGU方法，通过利用大语言模型和字幕的丰富文本信息来增强零样本时序动作定位中的细粒度动作判别能力，无需标注数据训练，在THUMOS14和ActivityNet-v1.3数据集上达到了当前最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有零样本时序动作定位方法依赖视觉-语言模型，但这类模型在细粒度动作分类上存在明显困难，难以区分动作的存在与否。多数现有方法需要在大规模视频数据集上训练，依赖标注数据且泛化能力有限。因此，本文旨在探索无需标注数据的方法，通过丰富文本信息来弥补监督不足。

Method: 提出TEGU（Textual Guidance for finer localization of actions in videos）方法，通过挖掘大语言模型生成的丰富文本信息以及从字幕中提取的结构化文本，为视频提供额外的语言上下文，从而增强对视频中细粒度动作差异的判别能力，实现无需训练数据的零样本时序动作定位。

Result: 在THUMOS14和ActivityNet-v1.3数据集上的实验结果表明，TEGU通过利用丰富文本信息改进动作定位性能，在无需训练的方法中超越了现有最优的零样本时序动作定位方法。

Conclusion: 利用大语言模型和字幕的结构化文本信息可以有效补偿标注数据的缺失，提升零样本时序动作定位中细粒度动作分类的性能，是一种有前景的无训练数据方案。

Original Abstract: Zero-shot temporal action localization (ZS-TAL) consists of classifying and localizing actions in untrimmed videos, where action classes are unseen at training time. Existing work uses Vision and Language Models (VLMs), taking advantage of their strong zero-shot transfer capabilities. Yet, these models face evident challenges with fine-grained action classification, making it difficult to directly use them to distinguish between the presence and absence of an action. Most current methods for ZS-TAL address these challenges by training models on large-scale video datasets, which require annotated data and often result in limited generalization performance. Recently, approaches discarding the use of labeled data have emerged as an alternative. Following this direction, we propose a novel approach, ``Textual Guidance for finer localization of actions in videos'' (TEGU), that compensates for the lack of supervision from training data by exploiting rich textual information derived from large language models and structured text extracted from captions. This additional linguistic context can improve fine-grained discrimination by providing richer cues about fine-grained action differences within videos. We validate the effectiveness of the proposed method by conducting experiments on the THUMOS14 and the ActivityNet-v1.3 datasets. Our results show that, by exploiting rich textual information for improved action localization, TEGU outperforms state-of-the-art ZS-TAL approaches that do not involve training

中文摘要翻译: 零样本时序动作定位（ZS-TAL）旨在未修剪视频中对动作进行分类和定位，其中动作类别在训练时是未见过的。现有工作利用视觉与语言模型，借助其强大的零样本迁移能力。然而，这些模型在细粒度动作分类上存在明显挑战，使其难以直接用于区分动作的存在与否。当前大多数ZS-TAL方法通过在大规模视频数据集上训练模型来解决这些问题，但这需要标注数据且通常导致泛化性能有限。最近，放弃使用标注数据的方法作为替代方案出现。沿着这一方向，我们提出了一种新颖方法——"文本引导的视频细粒度动作定位"（TEGU），它通过利用从大语言模型中导出的丰富文本信息以及从字幕中提取的结构化文本，来补偿训练数据监督的缺失。这种额外的语言上下文能够通过提供关于视频内部细粒度动作差异的更丰富线索，提升细粒度判别能力。我们在THUMOS14和ActivityNet-v1.3数据集上进行了实验，验证了所提方法的有效性。结果表明，通过利用丰富的文本信息改进动作定位，TEGU的性能超越了当前无需训练的零样本时序动作定位方法。

</details>


### [54] [EvoIR-Agent: Self-Evolving Image Restoration Agentic System via Experience-Driven Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22208)
*Kailin Zhuang,Jiawei Wu,Zhi Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EvoIR-Agent，一种自进化的训练无关图像恢复智能体，通过分层经验池和自进化机制，在不牺牲兼容性的前提下提升零样本规划性能，实现性能与效率的最佳Pareto平衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MLLM驱动的图像恢复智能体在退化耦合场景中灵活性强，但零样本规划缺乏经验导致严重试错开销。训练方法高效但缺乏对新工具/退化的兼容性，训练无关方法兼容性好但仍有试错开销，亟需一种既能高效规划又保持兼容性的方法。

Method: 1）系统形式化定义了训练无关图像恢复智能体的经验组件；2）构建分层经验池，提供从粗到细的多样化工具和移除顺序指导；3）引入自进化机制，从零开始利用累积记录更新经验池，持续提升性能与效率。

Result: 在全参考指标上显著领先，相比现有最先进方法实现了性能与效率之间卓越的Pareto最优平衡。

Conclusion: EvoIR-Agent通过自进化的分层经验池有效解决了训练方法与训练无关方法之间的两难困境，在图像恢复任务中同时实现了高性能与高效率。

Original Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM)-driven image restoration agent demonstrates effectiveness in degradation coupling scenarios by flexibly selecting tools and determining removal orders. However, their zero-shot planning often fails without experience, necessitating severe trial-and-error overhead to achieve satisfactory outcomes. Currently, two paradigms are employed to address this issue, yet a dilemma persists: Training-based methods embed intrinsic experience into parameters, achieving high inference efficiency but lacking compatibility with new tools or degradation. In contrast, training-free methods utilize explicit experience storage for compatibility but still incur trial-and-error overhead due to naive experience. To resolve the dilemma, we propose EvoIR-Agent, which first systematically formulates the experience components of a training-free image restoration agent. Subsequently, a hierarchical experience pool is constructed, which enables coarse-to-fine guidance for diverse tools and removal orders. Furthermore, a self-evolving mechanism is introduced to update the pool from scratch using accumulated records, thereby greatly improving performance and efficiency. Extensive experiments reveal that EvoIR-Agent achieves a significant lead in the full reference metrics and yields a remarkable Pareto-optimal balance between performance and efficiency compared to the state-of-the-art methods.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型驱动的图像恢复智能体通过灵活选择工具和确定去除顺序，在退化耦合场景中展现出有效性。然而，其零样本规划因缺乏经验而常常失败，需要严重的试错开销才能达到令人满意的结果。目前存在两种范式来解决这一问题，但仍存在困境：基于训练的方法将内在经验嵌入参数中，推理效率高但缺乏与新工具或新退化的兼容性；相反，训练无关的方法利用显式经验存储实现兼容性，但由于经验朴素，仍然会产生试错开销。为解决这一困境，我们提出EvoIR-Agent，它首先系统地形式化了训练无关图像恢复智能体的经验组件。随后，构建了一个分层经验池，能够为多样化的工具和去除顺序提供从粗到细的指导。此外，引入了一种自进化机制，利用累积记录从零开始更新经验池，从而大幅提升性能和效率。大量实验表明，EvoIR-Agent在全参考指标方面取得显著领先，并且与现有最先进方法相比，在性能和效率之间实现了卓越的帕累托最优平衡。

</details>


### [55] [A Robust Semantic Segmentation Pipeline for the CVPR 2026 8th UG2+ Challenge Track 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22216)
*Jinming Chai,Libo Yan,Licheng Jiao,Fang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对恶劣天气下的语义分割任务，提出了一种仅使用WeatherProof数据集的半监督分割流程。该方法以UniMatch V2为基线，将所有退化天气图像视为无标签数据进行半监督训练，并在推理时应用测试时增强，最终在CVPR 2026 UG2+挑战赛Track 2中取得良好效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 恶劣天气条件下的语义分割任务具有挑战性，现有方法往往依赖大量外部数据。本文旨在仅利用挑战赛提供的WeatherProof数据集，通过半监督学习充分挖掘数据分布，提升模型在退化天气图像上的分割性能。

Method: 采用UniMatch V2作为基线模型，将所有退化天气图像视为无标签数据进行半监督训练，以充分利用挑战赛提供的数据分布；推理阶段应用测试时增强（Test-Time Augmentation）提升预测的鲁棒性和分割精度。

Result: 该方法在CVPR 2026第8届UG2+挑战赛Track 2中提交并取得有效结果，代码已开源。

Conclusion: 仅利用WeatherProof数据集、不依赖任何外部数据，通过半监督学习和测试时增强即可有效应对恶劣天气下的语义分割挑战。

Original Abstract: This report presents our solution for the WeatherProof Dataset Challenge, namely CVPR 2026 8th UG2+ Challenge Track 2: Semantic Segmentation in Adverse Weather. For the semantic segmentation task under adverse weather conditions, we propose a semi-supervised segmentation pipeline. Our method is trained exclusively on the WeatherProof dataset, without using any additional external data. Specifically, we adopt UniMatch V2 as the baseline model and treat all degraded-weather images as unlabeled data for semi-supervised training, thereby fully exploiting the data distribution provided by the challenge. During inference, we further apply test-time augmentation to improve the robustness and segmentation accuracy of the final predictions. The code is publicly available at: https://github.com/ylb888/weatherproof-challenge-unimatchv2.

中文摘要翻译: 本报告展示了我们针对WeatherProof数据集挑战赛（即CVPR 2026第八届UG2+挑战赛Track 2：恶劣天气下的语义分割）所提出的解决方案。针对恶劣天气条件下的语义分割任务，我们提出了一种半监督分割流程。我们的方法仅在WeatherProof数据集上训练，未使用任何额外的外部数据。具体而言，我们采用UniMatch V2作为基线模型，将所有退化天气图像视为无标签数据进行半监督训练，从而充分利用挑战赛提供的数据分布。在推理阶段，我们进一步应用测试时增强来提升最终预测的鲁棒性和分割精度。代码已公开于：https://github.com/ylb888/weatherproof-challenge-unimatchv2。

</details>


### [56] [REACH: Hand Pose Estimation from Room Corners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22231)
*Shu Nakamura,Ryo Kawahara,Genki Kinoshita,Ryosuke Hirai,Yasutomo Kawanishi,Shohei Nobuhara,Ko Nishino*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出REACH-Net，一种基于Transformer的3D手部姿态估计方法，利用手-身体协调性、时间序列和多视角观察，从远处低分辨率且频繁遮挡的视角中准确恢复手部形状和姿态，并配套发布了大规模数据集REACH。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D手部姿态估计方法在远距离、低分辨率、频繁遮挡的固定摄像头场景（如房间角落摄像头）下表现不佳，缺乏同时考虑手-身体协调性、时间动态和多视角信息的方法。

Method: 提出基于Transformer的REACH-Net模型：通过每视角标记（per-view tokens）建模手和身体配置的视觉特征相关性，并以自回归方式利用时间协调性。同时构建了REACH数据集，使用隐蔽的胸部摄像头采集50名参与者在日常活动中的准确手部运动数据。

Result: 在REACH数据集上的大量实验（包括与现有方法的对比）表明，REACH-Net在远距离3D手部姿态估计中达到了高精度，优于已有方法。

Conclusion: 该工作拓展了3D手部姿态估计的边界，尤其推动了"野外"连续人类行为分析的发展。

Original Abstract: We introduce a novel 3D hand pose estimator that can accurately recover the shape and pose of people's hands in a room from afar, typically from fixed cameras at room corners, in extremely low-resolution and frequently occluded views. Our key idea is to fully leverage hand-body coordination, its temporal progression, and multiview observations. We achieve this with a novel Transformer-based model, in which hand and body configurations are modeled through correlations between their visual features expressed as per-view tokens, and their temporal coordination is exploited in an autoregressive manner. We introduce a novel dataset, which we refer to as REACH, Room-Environment dataset Annotated with Chest cameras for Hand pose estimation, to train and test our method. REACH is a first-of-its-kind large-scale hand pose dataset that captures accurate hand movements of 50 participants across a wide variety of daily activities. In order to avoid interfering with natural movements while annotating the hands with accurate shape and pose, we leverage concealed chest cameras. Through extensive experiments, including comparative studies with existing methods, we show that our model, REACH-Net, achieves highly accurate 3D hand pose estimation from afar. These results broaden the horizon of 3D hand pose estimation, especially towards "in-the-wild" continuous human behavior analysis.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了一种新颖的3D手部姿态估计器，能够从远处（通常来自房间角落的固定摄像头）在极低分辨率和频繁遮挡的视角下准确恢复人手形状和姿态。我们的关键思想是充分利用手-身体协调性、时间进程和多视角观察。我们通过一种新颖的基于Transformer的模型实现这一点，在该模型中，手和身体配置通过每视角标记表达的视觉特征之间的相关性进行建模，其时间协调性以自回归方式加以利用。我们引入了一个名为REACH的新数据集——利用胸部摄像头标注的房间环境手部姿态估计数据集——用于训练和测试我们的方法。REACH是首个大规模手部姿态数据集，捕捉了50名参与者在各种日常活动中准确的手部运动。为了在标注手部准确形状和姿态时不干扰自然运动，我们利用了隐蔽的胸部摄像头。通过大量实验（包括与现有方法的对比研究），我们证明了我们的模型REACH-Net在远距离3D手部姿态估计中实现了高精度。这些结果拓展了3D手部姿态估计的视野，尤其推动了"野外"连续人类行为分析的发展。

</details>


### [57] [D3Seg: Dependency-Aware Diffusion for Brain Tumor Segmentation with Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22249)
*Danish Ali,Ajmal Mian,Naveed Akhtar,Ghulam Mubashar Hassan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出D3Seg模型，通过多跳模态图融合、轻量扩散插补和概率空间决策细化，在多参数MRI缺失模态下实现稳定的脑肿瘤分割，在BraTS 2023上优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 临床中完整获取所有MRI序列不可行，现有分割方法在模态缺失时依赖简单特征拼接或直接融合策略，导致性能严重下降，亟需在缺失模态下保持稳定性能的分割方法。

Method: 提出D3Seg模型，包含三个核心组件：(1) 多跳模态图融合(MMGF)，建模高阶模态间依赖关系；(2) 轻量级扩散插补机制，在潜在空间中补偿缺失的T1ce表征；(3) 概率空间决策细化，缓解主导类别的过度自信并改善欠表征肿瘤子区域的分割。

Result: 在BraTS 2023数据集上，在多种缺失模态配置下，相比当前最优模型，增强肿瘤(ET)Dice提升约1.5-2.0%，肿瘤核心(TC)Dice提升约1.0%，同时保持计算效率。

Conclusion: D3Seg通过在缺失模态条件下引入高阶模态关系建模、潜在空间插补和决策细化，有效提升了分割性能与鲁棒性，为临床实际应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Accurate brain tumor segmentation using multiparametric MRI is critical for effective treatment planning. However, in clinical settings, complete acquisition of all MRI sequences is not always possible. The absence of certain MRI modalities results in substantial performance degradation in existing segmentation methods, which typically rely on naive feature concatenation or direct fusion strategies. To address this limitation, we propose a novel segmentation model D3Seg which is designed to maintain stable performance under missing-modality settings. D3Seg introduces Multi-hop Modality Graph Fusion (MMGF) to model higher order inter-modality dependencies, a lightweight diffusion-based imputation mechanism to compensate for missing T1ce representations in latent space, and probability-space decision refinement to mitigate dominant class overconfidence and improve delineation of underrepresented tumor subregions. Extensive evaluation on BraTS 2023 dataset demonstrates that our D3Seg model consistently improves segmentation performance under missing modality configurations. The proposed model achieves approximately 1.5-2.0% Dice improvement on enhancing tumor (ET) and around 1.0% on tumor core (TC) across multiple missing modality configurations compared to the current state-of-the-art model, while maintaining computational efficiency.

中文摘要翻译: 准确使用多参数MRI进行脑肿瘤分割对于有效治疗规划至关重要。然而，在临床环境中，完整采集所有MRI序列并非总是可行。某些MRI模态的缺失会导致现有分割方法性能显著下降，这些方法通常依赖于简单的特征拼接或直接融合策略。为解决这一限制，我们提出了一种新颖的分割模型D3Seg，旨在缺失模态设置下保持稳定的性能。D3Seg引入了多跳模态图融合(Multi-hop Modality Graph Fusion, MMGF)来建模高阶模态间依赖关系，一种轻量级的基于扩散的插补机制以在潜在空间中补偿缺失的T1ce表征，以及概率空间决策细化以缓解主导类别的过度自信并改善欠表征肿瘤子区域的分割。在BraTS 2023数据集上的广泛评估表明，我们的D3Seg模型在多种缺失模态配置下持续提升了分割性能。与当前最先进模型相比，所提模型在多种缺失模态配置下增强肿瘤(ET)的Dice系数提升了约1.5-2.0%，肿瘤核心(TC)提升了约1.0%，同时保持了计算效率。

</details>


### [58] [Direct content-based retrieval from music scores images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22255)
*Noelia Luna-Barahona,Antonio Ríos-Vila,David Rizo,Jorge Calvo-Zaragoza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了乐谱图像的内容检索方法，比较了基于OMR转录的方法、无转录的Transformer模型和文本提示的大语言模型，发现OMR方法在域内检索中表现更优，而无转录模型在处理跨域变体时更具鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 乐谱数字化虽有助于保存和访问，但信息检索仍主要依赖元数据搜索（如标题或作曲家），基于内容的乐谱图像检索尚未得到充分探索，而它在音乐家、音乐学家和教育工作者中具有潜在价值。

Method: 首先研究乐谱中哪些特征对搜索最相关，并定义了一种从任何标注语料库构建查询数据集的系统方法；然后比较了三种方法：基于光学音乐识别(OMR)的转录方法、基于Transformer的无转录模型（直接从乐谱图像识别查询）、以及基于文本提示的大语言模型。

Result: 在四个具有不同数据集规模、图像质量和排版机制的语料库上进行评估，结果表明：每种方法在不同条件下各有优势，OMR流水线在域内检索中表现更好，而无转录模型在处理域变体时更为有效。

Conclusion: 乐谱图像的内容检索没有单一的普适最优方法，OMR方法在域内场景中表现优异，而无转录方法更适合处理跨域变异性，研究为乐谱内容的系统化检索提供了方法论基础。

Original Abstract: The digitization of musical scores plays a crucial role in their preservation and accessibility, yet information retrieval still depends mainly on metadata searches, such as by title or composer. Content based search in music score images remains underexplored compared to text documents, despite its potential value for musicians, musicologists, and educators. This work contributes to the field by first studying which characteristics of a score are most relevant for search and by defining a systematic method to build query datasets from any annotated corpus. We also consider diverse methods for content-based search on music score images, ranging from transcription-based approaches relying on Optical Music Recognition (OMR), to a transcription-free Transformer model trained to recognize queries directly from score images, and a text-prompted Large Language Model. Our experiments evaluate these models on four corpora exhibiting diverse characteristics in terms of dataset size, image quality, and typesetting mechanisms. Overall, each method excels under different conditions: OMR-based pipelines achieve higher in-domain retrieval, whereas transcription-free models handle domain variability more effectively.

中文摘要翻译: 乐谱数字化对其保存和可访问性起着关键作用，然而信息检索仍主要依赖元数据搜索（如标题或作曲家）。与文本文档相比，乐谱图像中的内容搜索尽管对音乐家、音乐学家和教育工作者具有潜在价值，但仍未得到充分探索。本文首先研究了乐谱中哪些特征对搜索最为相关，并定义了一种从任何标注语料库构建查询数据集的系统方法，为该领域做出了贡献。我们还考虑了多种基于内容的乐谱图像搜索方法，包括依赖光学音乐识别（OMR）的转录方法、训练用于直接从乐谱图像识别查询的无转录Transformer模型，以及基于文本提示的大语言模型。我们在四个具有不同数据集规模、图像质量和排版机制的语料库上评估了这些模型。总体而言，每种方法在不同条件下各有优势：基于OMR的流水线在域内检索中表现更佳，而无转录模型在处理域变体时更为有效。

</details>


### [59] [MuKV: Multi-Grained KV Cache Compression for Long Streaming Video Question-Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22269)
*Junbin Xiao,Jiajun Chen,Tianxiang Sun,Xun Yang,Angela Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MuKV提出了一种多粒度KV缓存压缩模块和半层次化检索方法，在长期流式视频问答中同时提升准确率和效率，通过补丁级、帧级和片段级的多层次视觉表示压缩，并利用自注意力与频率引导的双信号令牌压缩机制，显著优于现有基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有流式视频问答方法缓存每一或两帧，导致冗余内存消耗，且丢失帧内细粒度空间细节和帧间时态上下文信息。

Method: MuKV包含两部分：(1) 多粒度KV缓存压缩模块，提取补丁级、帧级和片段级视觉表示，并通过自注意力与频率引导的双信号令牌压缩机制实现高效压缩；(2) 半层次化检索方法，在在线问答阶段检索相关KV缓存用于答案生成。

Result: 在长流式视频问答基准上，MuKV显著提升了答案准确率，同时不牺牲内存和在线问答效率；其压缩机制在准确率、内存和问答效率上均一致优于基线方法。

Conclusion: MuKV通过多粒度压缩和半层次化检索，有效解决了长流式视频问答中视觉令牌增长与LLM推理长度受限的矛盾，实现了准确率与效率的双重提升。

Original Abstract: Long streaming video QA remains challenging due to growing visual tokens and limited reasoning length of large language models (LLMs). KV-caching stores the Key-Value (KV) of the historical tokens via LLM prefill and enables more efficient streaming QA. However, existing methods cache every one or two frames, causing redundant memory usage and losing fine-grained spatial details within frame or temporal contexts across frames. This paper proposes MuKV, a method that features a multi-grained KV cache compression module and a semi-hierarchical retrieval approach to improve both efficiency and accuracy for long streaming VideoQA.
